ut.right = true;

            if ((code === 'KeyE' || code === 'Space') && !e.repeat && !(code === 'Space' && window.Story?.isDialogueActive)) {
                this.triggerInteract();
            }

            if (code === 'KeyH') window.MissionUI?.showHelp();
            if (code === 'KeyL') window.MissionUI?.showActivities();
            if (code === 'KeyG') window.MissionUI?.showMissionLinks();
            if (code === 'KeyM') window.MissionUI?.showMap();

            if (code === 'KeyJ') {
                window.Journal.toggle();
            }

        });

        window.addEventListener('keyup', (e) => {
            const code = e.code;
            if (['ArrowUp','ArrowDown','ArrowLeft','ArrowRight','Space'].includes(code)) e.preventDefault();
            if (e.target?.matches?.('input,textarea,select')) return;
            if (code === 'KeyW' || code === 'ArrowUp') this.input.up = false;
            if (code === 'KeyS' || code === 'ArrowDown') this.input.down = false;
            if (code === 'KeyA' || code === 'ArrowLeft') this.input.left = false;
            if (code === 'KeyD' || code === 'ArrowRight') this.input.right = false;
        });
    }

    setupTouchControls() {
        const joyBase = document.getElementById('touchJoyBase');
        const joyStick = document.getElementById('touchJoyStick');
        const btnInteract = document.getElementById('touchInteractBtn');

        if (!joyBase || !joyStick) return;

        let touchId = null;
        let startX = 0, startY = 0;

        joyBase.addEventListener('touchstart', (e) => {
            e.preventDefault();
            const touch = e.changedTouches[0];
            touchId = touch.identifier;
            const rect = joyBase.getBoundingClientRect();
            startX = rect.left + rect.width / 2;
            startY = rect.top + rect.height / 2;
            this.input.joystickActive = true;
        }, { passive: false });

        window.addEventListener('touchmove', (e) => {
            for (let i = 0; i < e.changedTouches.length; i++) {
                const touch = e.changedTouches[i];
                if (touch.identifier === touchId) {
                    const dx = touch.clientX - startX;
                    const dy = touch.clientY - startY;
                    const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
                    const maxDist = 45;
                    const angle = Math.atan2(dy, dx);
                    const clampedDist = Math.min(dist, maxDist);

                    joyStick.style.transform = `translate(${Math.cos(angle) * clampedDist}px, ${Math.sin(angle) * clampedDist}px)`;

                    this.input.joyX = (Math.cos(angle) * clampedDist) / maxDist;
                    this.input.joyY = (Math.sin(angle) * clampedDist) / maxDist;
                }
            }
        });

        const endTouch = (e) => {
            for (let i = 0; i < e.changedTouches.length; i++) {
                if (e.changedTouches[i].identifier === touchId) {
                    touchId = null;
                    joyStick.style.transform = `translate(0px, 0px)`;
                    this.input.joystickActive = false;
                    this.input.joyX = 0;
                    this.input.joyY = 0;
                }
            }
        };

        window.addEventListener('touchend', endTouch);
        window.addEventListener('touchcancel', endTouch);

        if (btnInteract) {
            btnInteract.addEventListener('touchstart', (e) => {
                e.preventDefault();
                this.triggerInteract();
            }, { passive: false });
        }
    }

    triggerInteract() {
        // An ordinary character recording must not disable mission interactions.
        if (window.Journal.isOpen || document.querySelector('.task-overlay, .mission-guide-overlay')) return;

        const preferNpc = this.nearbyNPC && (!this.nearbyInteractable || this.nearbyNPCDist <= this.nearbyInteractableDist + 14);
        if (preferNpc) {
            if(window.Story?.isDialogueActive)return; // Do not queue another NPC over an active speaker.
            window.Story.queueDialogue([
                { speaker: this.nearbyNPC.name, avatar: this.nearbyNPC.portrait, text: this.nearbyNPC.dialogue }
            ]);
            return;
        }

        if (this.nearbyInteractable) {
            const item = this.nearbyInteractable;
            if (item.type === 'minigame') {
                if (item.minigameId === 'dsnAlign' && window.Story?.completedTasks?.has('dsnAlign')) {
                    window.MissionUI?.notify('📡 Communications are already restored. Nice work!');
                    return;
                }
                window.Minigames.startTask(item.minigameId, (id) => {
                    window.Story.onTaskCompleted(id);
                });
            } else if (item.type === 'info') {
                if (item.infoId === 'stationTelemetry') {
                    // The first activation *only* triggers the existing Act 2 crew briefing.
                    // The player opens the telemetry display with a distinct, later E press.
                    if (!window.Story.completedTasks.has('stationTelemetry')) {
                        window.Story.onTaskCompleted('stationTelemetry');
                        window.Save?.schedule?.();
                        return;
                    }
                    if (window.Story.isDialogueActive) {
                        window.MissionUI?.notify?.('Finish or skip the crew briefing, then press E again to open telemetry.');
                        return;
                    }
                    window.Journal.open();
                    window.Journal.renderTab('telemetry');
                } else if (item.infoId === 'crewLogs') {
                    window.Journal.open();
                    window.Journal.renderTab('crew');
                } else if (item.infoId === 'lunarObservation') {
                    window.Observatory?.open();
                }
            }
            return;
        }
    }

    openObservationViewer() {
        if (document.querySelector('.observation-overlay')) return;
        if (window.Audio) window.Audio.uiClick();
        const earthSrc = window.SpaceArt?.earthSrc || '';
        const moonSrc = window.SpaceArt?.urls?.moon || earthSrc;
        const overlay = document.createElement('div');
        overlay.className = 'task-overlay observation-overlay';
        overlay.innerHTML = `
            <div class="task-modal polished-task-modal observation-modal" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="Observation viewer">
                <div class="task-header">
                    <div class="task-title-group">
                        <span class="task-icon">🔭</span>
                        <div>
                            <h2>LUNAR TELESCOPE VIEWER</h2>
                            <p class="task-subtitle">A cleaner, more realistic Moon-window explainer using reusable space assets instead of hand-drawn planets.</p>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    <button class="task-close-btn" id="obsCloseBtn">&times;</button>
                </div>
                <div class="task-body observation-body">
                    <div class="observation-layout">
                        <div class="observation-scene">
                            <div class="obs-asset-scene" aria-hidden="true">
                                <div class="obs-info-chip top">OPEN-SOURCE / PUBLIC-DOMAIN ASSETS</div>
                                <img class="obs-earth-asset" src="${earthSrc}" alt="" draggable="false">
                                <img class="obs-moon-horizon" src="${moonSrc}" alt="" draggable="false">
                                <div class="obs-window-ring"></div>
                                <div class="obs-focus-reticle"></div>
                                <div class="obs-window-caption">Observation dome</div>
                                <div class="obs-lunar-rim"></div>
                                <div class="obs-info-chip bottom">EARTHRISE STUDY VIEW</div>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                        <div class="observation-side">
                            <div class="observation-card">
                                <h4>What am I seeing?</h4>
                                <p>You are looking out from the Moon. The Earth asset is based on the reusable station art system, while the lunar foreground uses the same Moon asset family so the scene feels cleaner and more consistent.</p>
                            </div>
                            <div class="observation-kpi-row">
                                <div class="observation-kpi"><strong>384,400 km</strong><span>Average Earth–Moon distance</span></div>
                                <div class="observation-kpi"><strong>1.28 s</strong><span>One-way radio travel time</span></div>
                                <div class="observation-kpi"><strong>1/6 g</strong><span>Moon gravity compared with Earth</span></div>
                            </div>
                            <div class="observation-card">
                                <h4>Why it matters in the game</h4>
                                <ul>
                                    <li>Signal delay matters, so antenna alignment and communication tasks feel meaningful.</li>
                                    <li>Crews use outside observations to judge terrain, sunlight, and where safe travel is possible.</li>
                                    <li>The same visual language now matches the rest of the station better instead of mixing in rougher planet drawings.</li>
                                </ul>
                            </div>
                            <div class="observation-card">
                                <h4>Simple NASA note</h4>
                                <p>This is still a kid-friendly teaching scene, not a live telescope feed. The science ideas are real; the presentation is simplified for gameplay and clarity.</p>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>`;
        document.body.appendChild(overlay);
        overlay.querySelector('#obsCloseBtn')?.addEventListener('click', ()=>overlay.remove());
        overlay.addEventListener('click', (e)=>{ if (e.target === overlay) overlay.remove(); });
    }


    loop(currentTime) {
        const dt = Math.min(0.1, (currentTime - this.lastTime) / 1000.0);
        this.lastTime = currentTime;

        // A full-screen expedition or modal owns the screen. Do not keep rendering
        // the large lunar station behind it; that used to waste CPU/GPU and cause
        // noticeable stutter on low-power phones. Its canvas stays as a static frame.
        // The station clock is also paused while another activity is open.
        if (!document.hidden && !document.querySelector('.task-overlay, .mission-guide-overlay')) {
            this.update(dt);
            this.render();
        }

        requestAnimationFrame((t) => this.loop(t));
    }

    update(dt) {
        // 1. Advance fictional mission clock at a deterministic fixed-step rate.
        if (!document.hidden && !window.Minigames.activeMinigame && !window.Journal.isOpen && !document.querySelector('.task-overlay, .mission-guide-overlay')) {
            this._simAccumulator = (this._simAccumulator || 0) + dt;
            while (this._simAccumulator >= 0.1) { window.Sim.update(12); this._simAccumulator -= 0.1; }
        }

        // 2. Update Map (Doors, animated fans, holographic globe)
        window.StationMap.update(dt, this.player.x, this.player.y);

        // 3. Update Player & Companion
        const uiBlocked = !!document.querySelector('.mission-guide-overlay, .task-overlay') || window.Journal.isOpen;
        if (uiBlocked) { this.player.vx=0; this.player.vy=0; }
        this.player.update(dt, uiBlocked ? {up:false,down:false,left:false,right:false,joystickActive:false} : this.input, window.StationMap);
        this.pix.update(dt);

        // 4. Update Camera Lerp (Smooth following with deadzone)
        const targetCamX = this.player.x - this.camera.w / 2;
        const targetCamY = this.player.y - this.camera.h / 2;
        this.camera.x += (targetCamX - this.camera.x) * Math.min(1.0, dt * 6.0);
        this.camera.y += (targetCamY - this.camera.y) * Math.min(1.0, dt * 6.0);

        // Screen shake decay
        if (this.screenShake > 0) {
            this.screenShake = Math.max(0, this.screenShake - dt * 10);
            this.camera.x += (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * this.screenShake;
            this.camera.y += (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * this.screenShake;
        }

        // 5. Check Proximity to interactables and crew separately.
        this.nearbyInteractable = window.StationMap.getNearbyInteractable(this.player.x, this.player.y);
        this.nearbyInteractableDist = this.nearbyInteractable ? Math.hypot(this.player.x - this.nearbyInteractable.x, this.player.y - this.nearbyInteractable.y) : Infinity;
        let nearestCrew = null;
        let nearestCrewDist = Infinity;
        for (const npc of this.crew.npcs) {
            const dist = Math.hypot(this.player.x - npc.x, this.player.y - npc.y);
            if (dist < nearestCrewDist) {
                nearestCrew = npc;
                nearestCrewDist = dist;
            }
        }
        this.nearbyNPC = nearestCrewDist <= 96 ? nearestCrew : null;
        this.nearbyNPCDist = this.nearbyNPC ? nearestCrewDist : Infinity;

        // 6. Update Active Room Label
        const currentRoom = window.StationMap.getRoomAt(this.player.x, this.player.y);
        if (currentRoom) {
            this.activeRoomName = currentRoom.name;
        }

        // 7. Update HTML HUD Elements
        this.updateHUD();
        if (window.MissionUI) window.MissionUI.update();
    }

    updateHUD() {
        const hudPower = document.getElementById('hudPowerVal');
        const hudAir = document.getElementById('hudAirVal');
        const hudSafety = document.getElementById('hudSafetyVal');
        const roomEl = document.getElementById('hudRoomLabel');
        const objEl = document.getElementById('hudObjectiveText');
        const interactPrompt = document.getElementById('hudInteractPrompt');

        if (hudPower) {
            hudPower.textContent = `${window.Sim.hudPower}%`;
            hudPower.style.color = window.Sim.hudPower < 30 ? '#DA7468' : (window.Sim.hudPower < 60 ? '#E9AD65' : '#89BE96');
        }
        if (hudAir) {
            hudAir.textContent = `${window.Sim.hudAir}%`;
            hudAir.style.color = window.Sim.hudAir < 50 ? '#DA7468' : '#79C9D8';
        }
        if (hudSafety) {
            hudSafety.textContent = `${window.Sim.hudSafety}%`;
            hudSafety.style.color = window.Sim.hudSafety < 50 ? '#DA7468' : '#89BE96';
        }
        if (roomEl) {
            const worldTitle = ({moon:'LUNA-7',orbit:'ORBITAL LAB',mars:'ARES-1'})[window.StationMap?.currentWorld] || 'LUNA-7';
            roomEl.textContent = `${worldTitle} // ${this.activeRoomName.toUpperCase()}`;
        }
        if (objEl) {
            objEl.textContent = window.Story.activeObjective;
        }

        if (interactPrompt) {
            const preferNpc = this.nearbyNPC && (!this.nearbyInteractable || this.nearbyNPCDist <= this.nearbyInteractableDist + 14);
            if (preferNpc) {
                interactPrompt.classList.add('visible');
                interactPrompt.innerHTML = `<span class="key-pill">E</span> TALK TO <strong>${this.nearbyNPC.name.toUpperCase()}</strong>`;
            } else if (this.nearbyInteractable) {
                interactPrompt.classList.add('visible');
                interactPrompt.innerHTML = `<span class="key-pill">E</span> INTERACT WITH <strong>${this.nearbyInteractable.name.toUpperCase()}</strong>`;
            } else {
                interactPrompt.classList.remove('visible');
            }
        }
    }

    render() {
        const ctx = this.ctx;
        ctx.clearRect(0, 0, window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight);
        ctx.save();
        ctx.scale(this.zoom, this.zoom);

        // 1. Render Map Floors, Walls, and Consoles
        window.StationMap.render(ctx, this.camera);

        // 2. Render Station NPCs
        ctx.save();
        ctx.translate(-this.camera.x, -this.camera.y);
        this.crew.render(ctx);

        // 3. Render Player & PIX
        this.player.render(ctx);
        this.pix.render(ctx);
        ctx.restore();

        // 4. Subtle 2D Vignette & Atmospheric Radial Lighting
        this.renderAtmosphericLighting(ctx);
        this.renderWaypoint(ctx);
        ctx.restore();
    }


    /** Accessible compass drawn outside the world camera so distant destinations are always findable. */
    renderWaypoint(ctx) {
        const target = window.MissionUI?.getNavigationTarget(this.player) || window.MissionUI?.getTarget();
        if (!target || !this.player || document.querySelector('.task-overlay')) return;
        const dx=target.x-this.player.x, dy=target.y-this.player.y;
        const dist=Math.hypot(dx,dy);
        if(dist<90) return;
        const sx=this.camera.w/2, sy=this.camera.h/2;
        const a=Math.atan2(dy,dx);
        const radius=Math.min(135,Math.min(this.camera.w,this.camera.h)*.19);
        const x=sx+Math.cos(a)*radius,y=sy+Math.sin(a)*radius;
        ctx.save();
        ctx.translate(x,y);ctx.rotate(a);
        ctx.fillStyle='rgba(8,24,35,.86)';ctx.strokeStyle='#EFC48D';ctx.lineWidth=2;
        ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(25,0);ctx.lineTo(-14,-19);ctx.lineTo(-8,0);ctx.lineTo(-14,19);ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();ctx.stroke();
        ctx.restore();
    }

    renderAtmosphericLighting(ctx) {
        ctx.save();
        // Screen-edge cosmic dark vignette
        const grad = ctx.createRadialGradient(
            this.camera.w / 2, this.camera.h / 2, Math.min(this.camera.w, this.camera.h) * 0.4,
            this.camera.w / 2, this.camera.h / 2, Math.max(this.camera.w, this.camera.h) * 0.75
        );
        grad.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(11, 21, 32, 0)');
        grad.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(11, 21, 32, 0.4)');
        ctx.fillStyle = grad;
        ctx.fillRect(0, 0, this.camera.w, this.camera.h);

        // Emergency flashing red tint if solar storm active
        if (window.Sim.solarStormActive && !window.Sim.stormShelterShieldLocked) {
            const flash = (Math.sin(performance.now() / 250) + 1) * 0.08;
            ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(218, 116, 104, ${flash})`;
            ctx.fillRect(0, 0, this.camera.w, this.camera.h);
        }

        ctx.restore();
    }
}

// Start Game Engine on page load
window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
    window.Engine = new MoonshiftEngine();
    window.Engine.init();
});

</script>

  function installMapVariants(){
    const map = window.StationMap;
    if(!map || map._v16VariantsInstalled) return;
    map._v16VariantsInstalled = true;
    map.currentWorld = 'moon';
    map.width = 3400; map.height = 2400;
    map._applyVariantData = function(world){
      const set = ROOM_SETS[world] || ROOM_SETS.moon;
      this.currentWorld = world;
      this.rooms = {};
      for(const [id, room] of Object.entries(set.rooms)) this.rooms[id] = { id, ...deepClone(room) };
      this.corridors = deepClone(set.corridors);
      const LOGICAL_DOORS = {
        moon: ['d_dock_airlock','d_obs_greenhouse','d_obs_comms','d_shelter_comms'],
        orbit: ['d_dock_airlock','d_obs_greenhouse','d_obs_comms','d_shelter_comms'],
        mars: ['d_dock_airlock','d_obs_greenhouse','d_obs_comms','d_shelter_comms']
      };
      const keep = new Set(LOGICAL_DOORS[world] || []);
      this.doors = deepClone(set.doors).filter(d => keep.has(d.id)).map(d=>({ ...d, open:0, targetOpen:0, locked:false }));
      this.interactables = TASK_IDS.map(id => ({ id, ...deepClone(TASK_TEMPLATE[id]), ...deepClone(set.interactables[id]) }));
      this.roomFloorCache = {};
      this.backdropTexture = null;
      this.worldSpawn = deepClone(set.spawn);
    };
    map.applyWorld = function(world){
      this._applyVariantData(world);
      window.Engine?.setWorld?.(world);
      window.Story?.setWorld?.(world);
      window.CrewClass?.prototype?.setWorld && window.Engine?.crew?.setWorld(world);
      window.MissionUI?.clearMissionWaypoint?.();
    };
  }

  function patchCrew(){
    const proto = window.CrewClass && window.CrewClass.prototype;
    if(!proto || proto._v16Patched) return;
    proto._v16Patched = true;
    proto.setWorld = function(world){
      const set = ROOM_SETS[world] || ROOM_SETS.moon;
      for(const npc of this.npcs){
        const update = (set.crew || []).find(c=>c.id===npc.id);
        if(update) Object.assign(npc, deepClone(update));
      }
      this.currentWorld = world;
    };
  }

  function patchStory(){
    const story = window.Story;
    if(!story || story._v16Patched) return;
    story._v16Patched = true;
    story.world = story.world || 'moon';
    story.worldMeta = WORLD_META;
    const originalLoadAct = story.loadAct.bind(story);
    story.getChapterTitles = function(){ return STORY_PACKS[this.world]?.titles || ['Welcome aboard','The Moon Garden','Power puzzle','Breathe easy','Shelter challenge','The lost signal']; };
    story.setWorld = function(world){
      world = WORLD_META[world] ? world : 'moon';
      if(this.world === world) return;
      this.world = world;
      this.clearDialogueHandlers();
      this.completedTasks = new Set();
      this.dialogueQueue = [];
      this.isDialogueActive = false;
      this.endingShown = false;
      document.getElementById('dialogueBox')?.remove();
      this.currentAct = 1;
      this.activeObjective = '';
      this.loadAct(1);
      window.Save?.schedule?.();
    };
    story.loadAct = function(actNum){
      if((this.world || 'moon') === 'moon') return originalLoadAct(actNum);
      this.currentAct = actNum;
      window.Save?.schedule?.();
      const pack = STORY_PACKS[this.world] || STORY_PACKS.orbit;
      const scene = pack.acts[actNum];
      if(!scene) return;
      this.activeObjective = scene.objective;
      this.queueDialogue(scene.lines);
    };
    const originalTaskCompleted = story.onTaskCompleted.bind(story);
    story.onTaskCompleted = function(taskId){
      originalTaskCompleted(taskId);
      if(this.currentAct===6 && this.completedTasks.has('dsnAlign') && this.world!=='moon'){
        this.activeObjective = STORY_PACKS[this.world].complete;
      }
    };
  }

  function patchMissionUI(){
    const ui = window.MissionUI;
    if(!ui || ui._v16Patched) return;
    ui._v16Patched = true;
    ui.getTarget = function(){
      const s=window.Story;if(!s)return null;
      if(this.waypoint && this.waypoint.act===s.currentAct)return this.waypoint;
      if(this.isMissionComplete()) return null;
      const id=({1:'terminal_hab_holo',2:'task_greenhouse',3:'task_power',4:s.completedTasks.has('co2Scrubber')?'task_water':'task_scrubber',5:'task_shelter',6:'task_comms'})[s.currentAct];
      const item=window.StationMap?.interactables?.find(x=>x.id===id);
      return item?{x:item.x,y:item.y,label:(item.shortLabel||item.name||'TARGET').toUpperCase(),roomId:item.roomId,act:s.currentAct}:null;
    };
    const originalUpdate = ui.update.bind(ui);
    ui.update = function(){
      originalUpdate();
      const s=window.Story;
      const c=document.getElementById('chapterChip');
      if(c && s){
        const prefix = WORLD_META[s.world||'moon']?.chapterPrefix || 'LUNA-7';
        c.textContent = `✦ ${prefix} · CHAPTER ${s.currentAct} / 6`;
      }
    };
    ui.showMap = function(){
      const map=window.StationMap,player=window.Engine?.player;if(!map||!player)return;
      const target=this.getTarget();
      const colors={dock:'#79aec8',airlock:'#d6b68a',centralHab:'#8acdde',quarters:'#cfbc9d',shelter:'#da8e87',power:'#e9ad65',lifeSupport:'#76c7d5',greenhouse:'#91c99d',comms:'#79aec8',observation:'#b7d4de'};
      const roomRects=Object.values(map.rooms).map(r=>{const b=r.bounds,t=r.shortName||r.name.toUpperCase();const c=colors[r.id]||'#8dbacc';return `<g data-room="${r.id}" class="map-room" role="button" tabindex="0" aria-label="Navigate to ${t}"><rect x="${b.x}" y="${b.y}" width="${b.w}" height="${b.h}" rx="25" fill="${c}" fill-opacity=".16" stroke="${c}" stroke-width="13"/><rect x="${b.x+22}" y="${b.y+22}" width="${b.w-44}" height="${b.h-44}" rx="8" fill="none" stroke="${c}" stroke-opacity=".17" stroke-width="4"/><text x="${b.x+b.w/2}" y="${b.y+b.h/2}" fill="#e9f5ff" text-anchor="middle" font-family="Arial,sans-serif" font-weight="700" font-size="38">${t}</text></g>`}).join('');
      const corridors=map.corridors.map(c=>c.bend?`<path d="${map.corridorPathData(c)}" fill="none" stroke="#355a69" stroke-width="${c.bend.width}" stroke-linecap="butt"/>`:`<path d="${map.corridorPathData(c)}" fill="#30495a" stroke="none"/>`).join('');
      const marker=target?`<circle cx="${target.x}" cy="${target.y}" r="66" fill="rgba(239,186,120,.18)" stroke="#ffd69a" stroke-width="15" stroke-dasharray="32 22"/>`:'';
      const svg=`<svg id="stationMapSvg" viewBox="90 225 3200 2100" role="img" aria-label="Top down station map. Click a room to set your destination." xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"><rect width="3400" height="2400" fill="#0d1d2b"/>${corridors}${roomRects}${marker}<circle id="mapPlayerMarker" cx="${player.x}" cy="${player.y}" r="54" fill="#fff" stroke="#55d6e8" stroke-width="18"/><circle cx="${player.x}" cy="${player.y}" r="14" fill="#2a9eb5"/></svg>`;
      const worldTitle=WORLD_META[window.Story?.world||'moon']?.title || 'LUNA-7';
      const overlay=this.modal(`THE ${worldTitle.toUpperCase()} MAP`,`<p class="guide-lead">You are the blue-and-white dot. The <strong>gold circle</strong> is your next mission. Tap a room to choose your own destination.</p><div class="map-canvas-frame">${svg}</div><p class="map-caption" id="mapCaption">${target?'Current goal: '+target.label:'Explore the station.'}</p><div class="room-quick-picks">${Object.values(map.rooms).map(r=>`<button class="room-pick" data-room="${r.id}">${r.shortName||r.name.toUpperCase()}</button>`).join('')}</div><button class="task-action-btn map-go" id="mapGoBtn">LET'S GO!</button>`);
      overlay.querySelectorAll('.map-room,.room-pick').forEach(el=>{const select=()=>{const room=map.rooms[el.dataset.room];this.waypoint={x:room.bounds.x+room.bounds.w/2,y:room.bounds.y+room.bounds.h/2,label:(room.shortName||room.name).toUpperCase(),roomId:room.id,act:window.Story.currentAct};overlay.querySelector('#mapCaption').textContent=`Waypoint set: ${room.name}`;overlay.querySelectorAll('.map-room,.room-pick').forEach(c=>c.classList.remove('selected'));el.classList.add('selected');};el.onclick=select;el.onkeydown=e=>{if(e.key==='Enter'||e.key===' '){e.preventDefault();select()}};});
      overlay.querySelector('#mapGoBtn').onclick=()=>overlay.remove();
    };
  }

  function patchEngine(){
    const initPatch = ()=>{
      const engine = window.Engine; if(!engine || engine._v16Patched) return;
      engine._v16Patched = true;
      engine.setWorld = function(world){
        const spawn = (ROOM_SETS[world] || ROOM_SETS.moon).spawn;
        if(!this.player) return;
        this.clearMovementInput();
        Object.assign(this.player,{x:spawn.x,y:spawn.y,vx:0,vy:0,facing:'down',isMoving:false});
        this.camera.x = spawn.x - this.camera.w/2;
        this.camera.y = spawn.y - this.camera.h/2;
        this.pix.x = spawn.x - 40; this.pix.y = spawn.y - 40; this.pix.targetX = this.pix.x; this.pix.targetY = this.pix.y;
        this.crew?.setWorld?.(world);
        this.activeRoomName = window.StationMap?.getRoomAt(spawn.x,spawn.y)?.name || 'Station';
      };
      engine.crew?.setWorld?.(window.Story?.world || 'moon');
    };
    if(document.readyState==='loading') window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', ()=>setTimeout(initPatch,0));
    else setTimeout(initPatch,0);
  }

  function patchSave(){
    const save = window.Save;
    if(!save || save._v16Patched) return;
    save._v16Patched = true;
    const capture = save.capture.bind(save);
    save.capture = function(){ const data = capture(); if(data) data.world = window.Story?.world || 'moon'; return data; };
    const restore = save.restore.bind(save);
    save.restore = function(){
      let data = null;
      try { data = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(this.key)||'null'); } catch(e) {}
      const ok = restore();
      if(ok && data?.world && data.world!=='moon'){
        window.StationMap?.applyWorld?.(data.world);
        if(window.Story) window.Story.world = data.world;
        window.Engine?.crew?.setWorld?.(data.world);
      }
      return ok;
    };
  }

  function patchBeyond(){
    const beyond = window.Beyond;
    if(!beyond || beyond._v16Patched) return;
    beyond._v16Patched = true;
    beyond.activateWorld = function(id){
      document.querySelectorAll('.be-expedition-overlay').forEach(el=>el.remove());
      if(id==='moon'){
        window.StationMap?.applyWorld?.('moon');
        window.MissionUI?.notify?.('🌙 Back to the original LUNA-7 map.');
      } else {
        window.StationMap?.applyWorld?.(id);
        window.MissionUI?.notify?.(`✦ ${WORLD_META[id].title} loaded in the original map style.`);
      }
      this.updateHud?.();
    };
    const originalOpenHub = beyond.openHub.bind(beyond);
    beyond.openHub = function(){
      originalOpenHub();
      const overlay = document.querySelector('.be-hub-overlay');
      if(!overlay) return;
      overlay.querySelectorAll('[data-world]').forEach(btn=>{
        btn.disabled = false;
        btn.classList.add('is-unlocked');
        btn.classList.remove('is-locked');
        btn.onclick = ()=>{ const id = btn.dataset.world; overlay.remove(); this.activateWorld(id); };
      });
      const hdr = overlay.querySelector('.be-hub-header p + h2');
      if(hdr) hdr.textContent = 'Choose a World';
      const desc = overlay.querySelector('.be-hub-header p + h2 + p');
      if(desc) desc.textContent = 'Switch the original MOONSHIFT map to a Moon, Orbit or Mars version — same play style, different layout.';
    };
    beyond.startWorld = function(id){ this.activateWorld(id); };
  }

  function boot(){
    installMapVariants();
    patchCrew();
    patchStory();
    patchMissionUI();
    patchEngine();
    patchSave();
    patchBeyond();
    window.StationMap?.applyWorld?.(window.Story?.world || 'moon');
  }

  if(document.readyState==='loading') window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', boot);
  else boot();
})();

</script>
    <script>
/** MOONSHIFT 18 — world continuity and coherent space-engineering decisions. */
(() => {
  const STORE='moonshift.world-profiles.v18';
  const saved={};
  try { Object.assign(saved,JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(STORE)||'{}')); } catch (_) {}
  const allowedWorlds=['moon','orbit','mars'];
  const simKeys=['missionTimeHours','sunIllumination','solarEventMultiplier','solarStormActive','solarStormProgress','solarCapacityMax','solarGeneration','batteryCapacityMax','batteryCharge','powerAllocations','cabinPressure','oxygenLevel','co2Level','temperatureC','relativeHumidity','scrubberOperational','oxygenGeneratorOperational','potableWaterLitres','greywaterLitres','recyclingEfficiency','waterPurityPercent','greenhouseCropHealth','lightSpectrumRatio','moistureLevel','gardenWaterLitresPerHour','harvestProgress','exteriorRadiation','interiorRadiation','stormShelterShieldLocked','earthDistanceKm','lightSpeedKms','signalDelaySeconds','dsnLinkQuality','antennaAzimuth','antennaElevation','antennaAligned'];
  const saveProfiles=()=>{try{localStorage.setItem(STORE,JSON.stringify(saved))}catch(e){console.warn('World profiles unavailable',e)}};
  const record=world=>{
    if(!allowedWorlds.includes(world))return;
    let p=window.Save?.capture?.();
    if(!p)return;
    p.world=world;
    saved[world]=p;
    saveProfiles();
  };
  function restoreProfile(data){
    const sim=window.Sim, story=window.Story, player=window.Engine?.player;
    if(!data||!sim||!story||!player)return false;
    if(data.simulation){
      for(const k of simKeys){
        const v=data.simulation[k];if(v===undefined)continue;
        if(typeof sim[k]==='number'&&(!Number.isFinite(v)))continue;
        if(typeof v===typeof sim[k])sim[k]=typeof v==='object'&&v?JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(v)):v;
      }
    }
    sim.batteryCharge=Math.max(0,Math.min(sim.batteryCapacityMax,sim.batteryCharge));
    sim.updateAlerts();
    story.clearDialogueHandlers?.();
    story.dialogueQueue=[];story.isDialogueActive=false;
    document.getElementById('dialogueBox')?.remove();
    story.currentAct=Math.max(1,Math.min(6,Number(data.act)||1));
    story.activeObjective=String(data.objective||'Explore this outpost');
    story.completedTasks=new Set(Array.isArray(data.tasks)?data.tasks:[]);
    story.decisions=Array.isArray(data.decisions)?data.decisions:[];
    story.endingShown=!!data.endingShown;
    const x=Number(data.position?.x), y=Number(data.position?.y);
    if(Number.isFinite(x)&&Number.isFinite(y)&&window.StationMap?.getRoomAt(x,y)){
      player.x=x;player.y=y;
      window.Engine.camera.x=x-window.Engine.camera.w/2;
      window.Engine.camera.y=y-window.Engine.camera.h/2;
    }
    return true;
  }
  function freshSim(world){
    const sim=window.Sim;
    sim.reset();
    if(world==='orbit'){
      sim.sunIllumination=.88;
      sim.solarCapacityMax=48;
      sim.solarEventMultiplier=1;
      sim.batteryCharge=205;
      // LEO radio path is much shorter than the Earth–Moon path.
      sim.earthDistanceKm=600;
      sim.signalDelaySeconds=sim.earthDistanceKm/sim.lightSpeedKms;
      sim.exteriorRadiation=55; // fictional teaching scenario, not a dosimeter reading
    } else if(world==='mars'){
      sim.sunIllumination=.92;
      sim.solarCapacityMax=42;
      sim.solarEventMultiplier=.78; // fictional dust loss, not NASA measurement
      sim.batteryCharge=190;
      // Example one-way delay ~12.5 minutes at a scenario distance of 225 million km.
      sim.earthDistanceKm=225000000;
      sim.signalDelaySeconds=sim.earthDistanceKm/sim.lightSpeedKms;
      sim.exteriorRadiation=150; // fictional illustration
    }
    sim.update(0);
  }
  function switchWorld(world){
    if(!allowedWorlds.includes(world))return;
    const current=window.Story?.world||'moon';
    if(current!==world)record(current);
    document.querySelector('.be-hub-overlay')?.remove();
    window.StationMap?.applyWorld?.(world);
    if(current!==world){
      if(saved[world])restoreProfile(saved[world]);
      else freshSim(world);
    }
    window.MissionUI && Object.assign(window.MissionUI,{lastAct:0,renderStamp:0});
    window.Save?.save?.();
  }
  const PLAN={
    orbit:{
      greenhouseLight:{
        title:'Microgravity Lab',subtitle:'Tiny forces change motion in orbit.',icon:'✦',
        learn:'In orbit, objects and astronauts float because they are falling around Earth together. A small push changes motion; the item does not stop by itself.',
        reason:'Choose a plan to retrieve a drifting science kit without disturbing the station.',
        choices:[
          {label:'Gentle hand push',detail:'No electrical energy, but the kit could drift farther before anyone catches it.',energy:0,time:1,safety:-5},
          {label:'Tether and guide it',detail:'Spend a little station power and time to control the movement carefully.',energy:4,time:2,safety:4},
          {label:'Powerful robotic grab',detail:'Fast, but the robot draws significant power from the shared battery.',energy:12,time:0,safety:7}
        ],tip:'A safe, gentle force is often more useful than a big shove.'
      },
      stormShutter:{
        title:'Debris Readiness',subtitle:'Protect the orbiting station from a fictional debris alert.',icon:'✦',
        learn:'Small fragments can move very quickly in orbit. Crews monitor warnings, avoid unnecessary exposure, and prepare protected areas.',
        reason:'The station has limited energy and crew time. How should the team prepare?',
        choices:[
          {label:'Power the shielding actuators',detail:'Quick preparation, but spends battery reserve.',energy:12,time:0,safety:8},
          {label:'Manual inspection and stowage',detail:'Save some electricity but use more crew time.',energy:3,time:2,safety:6},
          {label:'Brief equipment check',detail:'Fast and cheap but leaves uncertainty about stored equipment.',energy:1,time:1,safety:-6}
        ],tip:'A good plan must protect the people and the equipment.'
      },
      dsnAlign:{
        title:'Earth Relay Link',subtitle:'Restore a low-Earth-orbit communication link.',icon:'✦',
        learn:'Radio signals travel at light speed. From a low orbit, a short one-way radio journey can take milliseconds, not the 1.28 seconds used in the Moon mission.',
        reason:'Choose how to restore the Earth relay while preserving battery.',
        choices:[
          {label:'Retry the same pointing',detail:'Very little energy, but still a weak signal.',energy:2,time:1,safety:-4},
          {label:'Calibrate the tracking antenna',detail:'Moderate energy and careful alignment.',energy:5,time:1,safety:6},
          {label:'Use full-power scanning',detail:'Fast scanning with a larger power cost.',energy:17,time:0,safety:4}
        ],tip:'Check alignment before using more power.'
      }
    },
    mars:{
      powerRouting:{
        title:'Dusty Solar Farm',subtitle:'Restore electricity under Martian dust.',icon:'✦',
        learn:'Mars receives less sunlight than Earth. Dust on a solar panel can make the available electrical output lower. Batteries help when solar power drops.',
        reason:'Your outpost needs energy for air, water and crops. What will you do?',
        choices:[
          {label:'Clean the panels',detail:'Crew time is used, then sunlight collection improves.',energy:4,time:2,safety:6,solarBoost:.18},
          {label:'Turn down lab loads',detail:'Protect the battery, but reduce science activity.',energy:1,time:1,safety:3,solarBoost:0},
          {label:'Run every machine',detail:'No interruption to science, but a serious energy cost.',energy:22,time:0,safety:-8,solarBoost:0}
        ],tip:'Dust storms do not automatically destroy solar panels, but less sunlight means a tighter budget.'
      },
      stormShutter:{
        title:'Dust Storm Shelter',subtitle:'Prepare the habitat for fine Martian dust.',icon:'✦',
        learn:'Martian dust is fine and can coat equipment. Dust control reduces contamination, but a dust storm is not the same as a radiation burst.',
        reason:'Choose a preparation that protects the air filters while respecting energy limits.',
        choices:[
          {label:'Seal all inlets and clean filters',detail:'Best protection, but requires some energy and crew effort.',energy:10,time:2,safety:9},
          {label:'Manual seal inspection',detail:'Lower battery demand; the crew needs more time.',energy:2,time:3,safety:5},
          {label:'Only close the outer hatch',detail:'Quick, but airborne dust may still affect equipment.',energy:1,time:1,safety:-7}
        ],tip:'The most thoughtful plan considers both electrical power and crew time.'
      },
      dsnAlign:{
        title:'Mars Relay',subtitle:'Send a message across interplanetary distance.',icon:'✦',
        learn:'Earth–Mars radio delay changes as the planets move. This game uses 225 million km as an example: about 12.5 minutes in one direction.',
        reason:'The antenna is connected; decide how to transmit an important science report.',
        choices:[
          {label:'Send a short, compressed packet',detail:'Low energy; fewer details get to Earth.',energy:3,time:1,safety:4},
          {label:'Schedule a verified transmission',detail:'More time, but good reliability and moderate energy.',energy:7,time:2,safety:8},
          {label:'Repeat an uncompressed full packet',detail:'High energy and time use without guaranteed benefit.',energy:20,time:3,safety:-4}
        ],tip:'Long delays make careful planning and message checks especially valuable.'
      }
    }
  };
  function patchGame(){
    if(window.MoonshiftQuality18Installed)return;
    window.MoonshiftQuality18Installed=true;
    const originalStart=window.Minigames.startTask.bind(window.Minigames);
    const originalGuide=window.Minigames.getGuideData.bind(window.Minigames);
    window.Minigames.getGuideData=function(key){
      const match=/^(orbit|mars)_(.+)$/.exec(key||'');
      if(!match)return originalGuide(key);
      const scene=PLAN[match[1]]?.[match[2]];
      if(!scene)return originalGuide(key);
      return {title:scene.title,summary:scene.learn,what:scene.reason,normal:'There is no single perfect setting. Read the choices and compare the costs.',low:'Not enough energy can stop other machines.',high:'Using too much power or time may reduce the reserve for tomorrow.',tip:scene.tip,steps:['Read the problem.','Compare each plan.','Pick one that meets the mission needs.','Observe the consequences.']};
    };
    window.Minigames.startTask=function(id,onComplete){
      const world=window.StationMap?.currentWorld||'moon';
      const scene=PLAN[world]?.[id];
      if(!scene)return originalStart(id,onComplete);
      this.onComplete=onComplete;
      this.currentTaskId=`${world}_${id}`;
      const modal=this.createModal(scene.title,scene.subtitle,scene.icon);
      const body=modal.querySelector('#taskBody');
      const hint=modal.querySelector('#taskHint');
      const action=modal.querySelector('#taskActionBtn');
      let selected=1;
      const render=()=>{
        const sim=window.Sim;
        body.innerHTML=`<div class="v18-science-intro"><strong>Why this matters</strong><p>${scene.learn}</p></div><div class="v18-sim-head">${scene.reason}</div><div class="v18-choices">${scene.choices.map((c,i)=>`<button type="button" data-choice="${i}" class="v18-choice ${selected===i?'selected':''}"><span class="v18-choice-title">${c.label}</span><span>${c.detail}</span><small>Battery −${c.energy} kWh · Crew time +${c.time} h</small></button>`).join('')}</div><p class="v18-reserve">Battery reserve: ${sim.batteryCharge.toFixed(0)} kWh · One possible training plan, not a NASA operational procedure</p>`;
        body.querySelectorAll('[data-choice]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{selected=Number(b.dataset.choice);render()});
      };
      render();hint.textContent='Choose a plan, then check its real resource cost.';action.textContent='APPLY MY PLAN';
      action.onclick=()=>{
        const sim=window.Sim,choice=scene.choices[selected];
        if(sim.batteryCharge+1e-6<choice.energy){hint.textContent='Not enough stored energy for that plan. Choose another strategy.';return;}
        sim.batteryCharge=Math.max(0,sim.batteryCharge-choice.energy);
        sim.missionTimeHours+=choice.time;
        if(choice.solarBoost)sim.solarEventMultiplier=Math.min(1,sim.solarEventMultiplier+choice.solarBoost);
        if(id==='stormShutter')sim.stormShelterShieldLocked=choice.safety>=0;
        if(id==='dsnAlign'){sim.antennaAligned=choice.safety>=0;sim.dsnLinkQuality=choice.safety>=0?90:30;}
        sim.update(0); window.Save?.schedule?.();window.Audio?.taskSuccess?.();
        this.close();
        onComplete?.(id);
        window.MissionUI?.notify?.(`${scene.title}: ${choice.label}. Battery −${choice.energy} kWh · Crew time +${choice.time} h.`);
      };
    };
    // Light micro-thruster-assisted floating in the Orbital Lab. No footsteps
    // in free fall; holding a direction supplies a gentle continuous acceleration.
    const playerProto=window.PlayerClass?.prototype;
    if(playerProto){
      const originalMove=playerProto.update;
      playerProto.update=function(dt,input,map){
        if((map?.currentWorld||'moon')!=='orbit')return originalMove.call(this,dt,input,map);
        let x=(input.right?1:0)-(input.left?1:0), y=(input.down?1:0)-(input.up?1:0);
        if(input.joystickActive){x=input.joyX;y=input.joyY;}
        const n=Math.hypot(x,y);
        if(n>0){x/=Math.max(1,n);y/=Math.max(1,n);this.vx+=x*740*dt;this.vy+=y*740*dt;this.facing=Math.abs(x)>Math.abs(y)?x>0?'right':'left':y>0?'down':'up';}
        const brake=Math.exp(-dt*(n>0?0.8:3.0));this.vx*=brake;this.vy*=brake;
        const speed=Math.hypot(this.vx,this.vy),maxSpeed=205;
        if(speed>maxSpeed){this.vx*=maxSpeed/speed;this.vy*=maxSpeed/speed;}
        const nx=this.x+this.vx*dt,ny=this.y+this.vy*dt;
        if(map.isPassable(nx,this.y,this.radius))this.x=nx;else this.vx=0;
        if(map.isPassable(this.x,ny,this.radius))this.y=ny;else this.vy=0;
        this.walkTimer=0;this.isMoving=false;
      };
    }
    // A separate saved game for each destination. Previous battery depletion must
    // never make a new world appear broken on arrival.
    const originalActivate=window.Beyond.activateWorld.bind(window.Beyond);
    window.Beyond.activateWorld=function(id){
      if(!allowedWorlds.includes(id))return;
      const previous=window.Story?.world||'moon';
      if(previous!==id)record(previous);
      originalActivate(id);
      if(previous!==id){
        if(saved[id])restoreProfile(saved[id]);else freshSim(id);
      }
      window.MissionUI && Object.assign(window.MissionUI,{lastAct:0,renderStamp:0});
      window.Save?.save?.();
    };
    const origReset=window.Save.reset.bind(window.Save);
    window.Save.reset=function(){
      for(const k of Object.keys(saved))delete saved[k];
      try{localStorage.removeItem(STORE);}catch(_){}
      const result=origReset();
      if(window.StationMap?.currentWorld!=='moon')window.StationMap.applyWorld('moon');
      return result;
    };
    const storeData=(()=>{try{return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(window.Save.key)||'null')}catch(_){return null}})();
    if(storeData?.world && storeData.world!=='moon'){
      window.StationMap?.applyWorld?.(storeData.world);
      restoreProfile(storeData);
      window.StationMap.currentWorld=storeData.world;
      window.Story.world=storeData.world;
      window.Save?.save?.();
    }
    // One objective card is sufficient. The secondary route chip fades away
    // after the player has understood the mission; the gold compass stays useful.
    const ui=window.MissionUI;
    if(ui){
      const prevUpdate=ui.update.bind(ui);
      ui._hintExpiry=performance.now()+6500;
      ui._hintAct=window.Story?.currentAct;
      ui._hintWorld=window.Story?.world;
      ui.update=function(){
        const currentAct=window.Story?.currentAct,world=window.Story?.world;
        const changed=(currentAct!==this._hintAct || world!==this._hintWorld);
        if(changed){this._hintAct=currentAct;this._hintWorld=world;this._hintExpiry=performance.now()+6500;}
        prevUpdate();
        const c=document.getElementById('destinationChip');
        if(c && performance.now()>this._hintExpiry && !c.classList.contains('is-complete'))c.style.display='none';
        if(changed && world!=='moon'){
          const titles=window.Story?.getChapterTitles?.();
          this.notify(`${world==='orbit'?'Orbital Lab':'ARES-1'} · ${titles?.[currentAct-1]||'Mission briefing'}`);
        }
      };
    }

    // Use world-specific information in the telescope/observation pop-up.
    const engine=window.Engine;
    if(engine){
      const moonViewer=engine.openObservationViewer.bind(engine);
      engine.openObservationViewer=function(){
        const world=window.StationMap?.currentWorld||'moon';
        if(world==='moon')return moonViewer();
        if(world==='orbit'){
          window.MissionUI?.modal('CUPOLA · EARTH OBSERVATION',`<div class="v18-science-intro"><strong>What are we seeing?</strong><p>From a low-Earth orbit, Earth looks enormous through the cupola window. Our view is an educational illustration, not a live camera feed.</p></div><div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:22px;flex-wrap:wrap;padding:20px 0"><img src="${window.SpaceArt.earthSrc}" width="142" height="142" alt="Illustrated Earth from public-domain coastline data"/><div style="flex:1;min-width:210px"><strong>Radio signal travel</strong><p>A 600 km one-way radio path takes about <b>0.002 seconds</b> at the speed of light. Actual station-to-ground routes depend on geometry and networks.</p><p>Orbiting astronauts are in continuous free fall—not outside Earth's gravity.</p></div></div><p class="science-note">Illustrative distances and illustrations; not a live NASA mission view.</p>`);
        } else {
          window.MissionUI?.modal('ARES-1 · ROVER WORKSHOP',`<div class="v18-science-intro"><strong>Plan your Mars expedition</strong><p>The rover needs enough battery for both its journey and return. Dust and uneven ground can change a route's risk.</p></div><div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:22px;flex-wrap:wrap;padding:20px 0">${window.SpaceArt.iconMarkup('rover','v18-rover-illustration')}<div style="flex:1;min-width:210px"><strong>Communication takes time</strong><p>At our example distance of 225 million km, a radio signal needs about <b>12.5 minutes each way</b>. The real delay changes with planetary positions.</p><p>Pick safe routes and send careful instructions; immediate joystick driving from Earth is unrealistic.</p></div></div><p class="science-note">Mars science based on real physical principles; route and mission budgets are simplified game assumptions.</p>`);
        }
      };
    }
    window.addEventListener('pagehide',()=>record(window.Story?.world||'moon'));
  }
  window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',patchGame);
})();

</script>
    <script>
/**
 * MOONSHIFT v19 — topology-driven passages.
 * Each corridor overlaps BOTH adjacent room interiors; sliding shutters
 * follow the actual passage tangent. Visuals and collision use one geometry.
 */
(function () {
  'use strict';
  const LINKS = [
    ['dock_hab','dock','centralHab','h'],
    ['dock_airlock','dock','airlock','v'],
    ['hab_quarters','quarters','centralHab','v'],
    ['hab_shelter','centralHab','shelter','bendUpper'],
    ['hab_power','centralHab','power','v'],
    ['hab_lifesupport','centralHab','lifeSupport','bendLower'],
    ['hab_obs','centralHab','observation','h'],
    ['obs_greenhouse','observation','greenhouse','v'],
    ['obs_comms','comms','observation','v'],
    ['shelter_comms','shelter','comms','h']
  ];
  const WIDTH=142, THICKNESS=13;
  const clamp=(v,lo,hi)=>Math.min(hi,Math.max(lo,v));
  function midpointInOverlap(a0,a1,b0,b1){
    const l=Math.max(a0,b0)+65, h=Math.min(a1,b1)-65;
    return h>=l?(l+h)/2:(Math.max(a0,b0)+Math.min(a1,b1))/2;
  }
  function straight(id,A,B,axis){
    const a=A.bounds,b=B.bounds;
    if(axis==='h'){
      let left=a,right=b;
      if(a.x>b.x){left=b;right=a;}
      const y=midpointInOverlap(left.y,left.y+left.h,right.y,right.y+right.h);
      const x0=left.x+left.w-58,x1=right.x+58;
      if(x1<x0+36)throw Error('Invalid horizontal passage '+id);
      return {id,x:x0,y:y-WIDTH/2,w:x1-x0,h:WIDTH,link:[A.id,B.id],axis};
    }
    let top=a,bottom=b;
    if(a.y>b.y){top=b;bottom=a;}
    const x=midpointInOverlap(top.x,top.x+top.w,bottom.x,bottom.x+bottom.w);
    const y0=top.y+top.h-58,y1=bottom.y+58;
    if(y1<y0+36)throw Error('Invalid vertical passage '+id);
    return {id,x:x-WIDTH/2,y:y0,w:WIDTH,h:y1-y0,link:[A.id,B.id],axis};
  }
  function bend(id,A,B,kind){
    const a=A.bounds,b=B.bounds;
    let start,end,c1,c2;
    if(kind==='bendUpper'){
      // From the habitat's upper-right shoulder to the shelter's lower-left.
      start=[a.x+a.w-98,a.y+82];
      end=[b.x+100,b.y+b.h-85];
      const midY=(start[1]+end[1])/2;
      c1=[start[0],midY+16];
      c2=[end[0],midY-16];
    }else{
      // From the habitat's lower-right shoulder to the recycler upper-left.
      start=[a.x+a.w-94,a.y+a.h-82];
      end=[b.x+110,b.y+87];
      const midY=(start[1]+end[1])/2;
      c1=[start[0],midY-15];
      c2=[end[0],midY+15];
    }
    const pts=[start,c1,c2,end];
    const minX=Math.min(...pts.map(p=>p[0]))-WIDTH/2-18;
    const minY=Math.min(...pts.map(p=>p[1]))-WIDTH/2-18;
    const maxX=Math.max(...pts.map(p=>p[0]))+WIDTH/2+18;
    const maxY=Math.max(...pts.map(p=>p[1]))+WIDTH/2+18;
    return {id,x:minX,y:minY,w:maxX-minX,h:maxY-minY,
      bend:{p0:start,p1:c1,p2:c2,p3:end,width:WIDTH},link:[A.id,B.id],axis:'bend'};
  }
  function midpointDoor(map,c){
    let cx,cy,angle;
    if(c.bend){
      const t=.51,b=c.bend;
      const p=map.cubicPoint(b,t);
      const u=1-t;
      const dx=3*u*u*(b.p1[0]-b.p0[0])+6*u*t*(b.p2[0]-b.p1[0])+3*t*t*(b.p3[0]-b.p2[0]);
      const dy=3*u*u*(b.p1[1]-b.p0[1])+6*u*t*(b.p2[1]-b.p1[1])+3*t*t*(b.p3[1]-b.p2[1]);
      cx=p.x;cy=p.y;
      angle=Math.atan2(dx,-dy);
    }else if(c.axis==='h'){
      cx=c.x+c.w/2;cy=c.y+c.h/2;angle=Math.PI/2;
    }else{
      cx=c.x+c.w/2;cy=c.y+c.h/2;angle=0;
    }
    const span=WIDTH-34;
    const dir={x:Math.cos(angle),y:Math.sin(angle)};
    // The extent rectangle is used only for compatibility with status/UI,
    // never to decide collision or render orientation.
    const ex=Math.abs(dir.x)*span/2+Math.abs(dir.y)*THICKNESS/2;
    const ey=Math.abs(dir.y)*span/2+Math.abs(dir.x)*THICKNESS/2;
    return {id:'d_'+c.id,x:cx-ex,y:cy-ey,w:ex*2,h:ey*2,
      cx,cy,angle,span,thickness:THICKNESS,locked:false,open:0,targetOpen:0,link:c.link};
  }
  function doorCoordinates(door,x,y){
    const dx=x-door.cx,dy=y-door.cy;
    const co=Math.cos(door.angle),si=Math.sin(door.angle);
    return {u:dx*co+dy*si,v:-dx*si+dy*co};
  }
  function build(map){
    const rooms=map.rooms;
    const corridors=LINKS.map(([id,a,b,axis])=>{
      if(!rooms[a]||!rooms[b])throw Error('Missing room for '+id);
      return axis.startsWith('bend')?bend(id,rooms[a],rooms[b],axis):straight(id,rooms[a],rooms[b],axis);
    });
    const doors=corridors.map(c=>midpointDoor(map,c));
    map.corridors=corridors;
    map.doors=doors;
    map._connectedWorld=map.currentWorld||'moon';
  }
  function install(){
    const map=window.StationMap;
    if(!map||map._v19TopologyInstalled)return;
    map._v19TopologyInstalled=true;
    const originalApply=map.applyWorld?.bind(map);
    map.applyWorld=function(world){
      if(originalApply)originalApply(world);
      build(this);
    };
    const oldUpdate=map.update.bind(map);
    map.update=function(dt,px,py){
      const current=this.doors;
      this.doors=[];
      oldUpdate(dt,px,py); // retain fan, hologram, animation state
      this.doors=current;
      for(const d of current){
        const {u,v}=doorCoordinates(d,px,py);
        const range=d.targetOpen?212:170;
        const du=Math.max(0,Math.abs(u)-d.span/2);
        const dv=Math.max(0,Math.abs(v)-d.thickness/2);
        const open=!d.locked && Math.hypot(du,dv)<range;
        if(open&&!d.targetOpen&&(!this._lastDoorChime||performance.now()-this._lastDoorChime>900)){
          window.Audio?.doorOpen?.();this._lastDoorChime=performance.now();
        }
        d.targetOpen=open?1:0;
        d.open+=(d.targetOpen-d.open)*Math.min(1,dt*10);
        if(Math.abs(d.targetOpen-d.open)<.008)d.open=d.targetOpen;
      }
    };
    const originalPassable=map.isPassable.bind(map);
    map.isPassable=function(x,y,radius=20){
      const active=this.doors;this.doors=[];
      let base;
      try{base=originalPassable(x,y,radius);}finally{this.doors=active;}
      if(!base)return false;
      for(const d of active){
        const {u,v}=doorCoordinates(d,x,y);
        if(Math.abs(v)>d.thickness/2+radius)continue;
        const pocket=Math.max(0,((d.span/2)-7)*(1-d.open));
        const left= -d.span/2+7;
        const right=d.span/2-7;
        if(d.locked){if(Math.abs(u)<d.span/2+radius)return false;}
        else if(pocket>0.3 && ((u>=left-radius&&u<=left+pocket+radius) ||
          (u>=right-pocket-radius&&u<=right+radius)))return false;
      }
      return true;
    };
    map.renderDoor=function(ctx,d){
      const len=d.span,t=d.thickness,progress=clamp(d.open,0,1),jamb=7;
      ctx.save();ctx.translate(d.cx,d.cy);ctx.rotate(d.angle);
      // Attached door pockets. The open state does NOT draw any bar across the passage.
      const anchor=len/2-2;
      for(const side of [-1,1]){
        const x=side*anchor;
        ctx.fillStyle='#263d49';ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(135,176,192,.40)';ctx.lineWidth=1;
        ctx.beginPath();ctx.roundRect(x-5,-t/2-11,10,t+22,4);ctx.fill();ctx.stroke();
        ctx.fillStyle=side<0?'#90b3b9':'#789aa2';
        ctx.fillRect(x-2,-t/2+1,2,t-2);
      }
      // Leaves slide sideways into the wall pockets.
      const leaf=Math.max(0,(len/2-jamb)*(1-progress));
      if(leaf>0.2){
        const metallic=ctx.createLinearGradient(0,-t/2,0,t/2);
        metallic.addColorStop(0,d.locked?'#866358':'#5e7b8a');
        metallic.addColorStop(.55,d.locked?'#684944':'#385664');
        metallic.addColorStop(1,'#243c4b');
        for(const side of [-1,1]){
          const x=side<0?-len/2+jamb:len/2-jamb-leaf;
          ctx.fillStyle=metallic;ctx.beginPath();ctx.roundRect(x,-t/2,leaf,t,3);ctx.fill();
          ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(185,213,218,.24)';ctx.lineWidth=.85;ctx.stroke();
        }
      }
      const light=d.locked?'#d78c83':progress>.75?'#90cab6':'#c8b185';
      ctx.fillStyle=light;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(-anchor,0,2.3,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();
      ctx.restore();
    };
    build(map);
  }
  window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',install);
})();

</script>
    <script>
(() => {
 const art=(n)=>window.MoonshiftIcons?.markup(n)||'';
 const sim=window.Sim;
 if(!sim)return;
 const originalReset=sim.reset.bind(sim);
 sim.reset=function(){originalReset();this.missionFailed=false;this.failureCount=0;document.querySelector('.v20-failure-overlay')?.remove();};
 sim.missionFailed=false;
 const originalUpdate=sim.update.bind(sim);
 sim.update=function(dt=1){
   if(this.missionFailed)return;
   originalUpdate(dt);
   if(dt>0&&this.batteryCharge<=.001&&this.powerShortfall&& !window.MoonshiftEnergy?.available){
     this.missionFailed=true;
     queueMicrotask(()=>showFailure());
   }
 };
 function showFailure(){
  if(!sim.missionFailed||document.querySelector('.v20-failure-overlay'))return;
  window.Engine?.clearMovementInput?.();
  document.querySelectorAll('.task-overlay:not(.v20-failure-overlay),.mission-guide-overlay,.be-hub-overlay').forEach(x=>x.remove());
  const overlay=document.createElement('div');
  overlay.className='task-overlay v20-failure-overlay';
  overlay.innerHTML=`<section class="v20-failure" role="alertdialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="v20failTitle" tabindex="-1">
    <div class="v20-failure-pic">${art('warning')}${art('battery')}</div>
    <p class="v20-eyebrow">MISSION RESULT · TRAINING SIMULATION</p>
    <h2 id="v20failTitle">Power failure — mission lost</h2>
    <p>The battery reached <strong>0 kWh</strong>, and the solar panels could not supply all essential machines. The crew's life-support systems can't continue operating safely under this plan.</p>
    <div class="v20-failure-stats"><span><small>Stored energy</small><strong>0 kWh</strong></span><span><small>Solar available</small><strong>${sim.solarGeneration.toFixed(1)} kW</strong></span><span><small>Machines request</small><strong>${sim.totalDemand.toFixed(1)} kW</strong></span></div>
    <div class="v20-failure-lesson">${art('power')}<div><strong>What did we learn?</strong><p>kW means how quickly electricity is used. kWh means energy stored. When demand is greater than supply, the battery empties. Try giving essential systems power first.</p></div></div>
    <button type="button" id="v20Retry" class="v20-retry">Retry from a safe checkpoint</button><small class="v20-retry-note">In this fictional training restart, your crew restores a 20% reserve and activates a safer power plan. Your completed chapters stay saved.</small>
  </section>`;
  document.body.appendChild(overlay);overlay.querySelector('section')?.focus();
  overlay.querySelector('#v20Retry').addEventListener('click',()=>{
    // REWIND the training scenario, not an instantaneous power generator.
    sim.batteryCharge=sim.batteryCapacityMax*.20;
    sim.powerAllocations.lifeSupport=12;
    sim.powerAllocations.thermalHeaters=3;
    sim.powerAllocations.greenhouse=2;
    sim.powerAllocations.communications=2;
    sim.powerAllocations.auxiliary=1;
    sim.missionFailed=false;
    sim.failureCount=(sim.failureCount||0)+1;
    sim.update(0);
    sim.updateAlerts();
    window.Save?.save?.();
    overlay.remove();
    window.MissionUI?.notify?.('Checkpoint restored. You have a 20% battery reserve and a safe starter plan.');
    window.Audio?.uiConfirm?.();
  });
 }
 // Explain the real difference between no stored battery and unavailable electricity.
 const ui=window.MissionUI;
 if(ui){
  ui.showMissionLinks=function(){
   const s=window.Sim,p=s.powerAllocations;
   const total=Object.values(p).reduce((a,b)=>a+Number(b),0);
   const short=total>s.solarGeneration;
   const card=(icon,title,value,desc)=>`<div class="v20-machine">${art(icon)}<div><strong>${title}</strong><span>${value}</span><small>${desc}</small></div></div>`;
   const body=`<div class="v20-systems-intro"><p class="v20-eyebrow">VISUAL SCIENCE EXPLORER</p><h3>Where does electricity go?</h3><p>Follow the equipment below. Each machine needs its share of the station's electricity.</p></div>
      <div class="v20-energy-strip">${card('solar','Solar panels',s.solarGeneration.toFixed(1)+' kW','Electricity available now')}${card('battery','Battery',Math.round(s.batteryCharge)+' / '+Math.round(s.batteryCapacityMax)+' kWh','Stored energy for later')}</div>
      <div class="v20-systems-label">FOUR JOBS THAT NEED POWER</div><div class="v20-machine-grid">${card('air','Clean the air',p.lifeSupport.toFixed(1)+' kW','Most important for breathing')}${card('plant','Grow our food',p.greenhouse.toFixed(1)+' kW','Plants need light')}${card('radio','Send messages',p.communications.toFixed(1)+' kW','Keep in touch with Earth')}${card('power','Keep warm',p.thermalHeaters.toFixed(1)+' kW','Habitat temperature')}</div>
      <div class="v20-systems-summary ${short?'is-risk':''}">${art(short?'warning':'solar')}<p><strong>${short?`Needs ${Math.abs(total-s.solarGeneration).toFixed(1)} kW more than solar provides`:`Solar covers the current load with ${(s.solarGeneration-total).toFixed(1)} kW spare`}</strong><span>${short?'The battery is draining. Reduce optional loads before it runs out.':'Extra electricity can recharge the battery until it is full.'}</span></p></div>
      <p class="v20-systems-note">These numbers are fictional engineering training values. Real NASA stations use more complex power and life-support systems.</p>
      <button type="button" class="v20-primary" id="v20ExplorePlan">Try your own resource plan</button>`;
   const modal=this.modal('THE STATION POWER SYSTEM',body);modal.classList.add('v20-system-overlay');
   modal.querySelector('#v20ExplorePlan')?.addEventListener('click',()=>{modal.remove();window.DecisionLab?.open();});
  };
 }
 // Reduce repeated screen decoration. The status bar is still accessible but not permanent.
 const baseUpdate=window.MissionUI?.update?.bind(window.MissionUI);
 if(baseUpdate){
  window.MissionUI.update=function(){baseUpdate();
    const s=window.Sim;
    const e=document.getElementById('conditionChip');
    if(e && s?.missionFailed){e.textContent='Mission lost — power outage';e.style.display='inline-flex';}
  };
 }
 // Keep reset from leaving a failure overlay on-screen.
 const reset=window.Save?.reset?.bind(window.Save);
 if(reset)window.Save.reset=function(...a){document.querySelector('.v20-failure-overlay')?.remove();return reset(...a);};
 window.PowerCrisis={show:showFailure};
})();

    <script>
(() => {
'use strict';
const world=()=>window.Story?.world || window.StationMap?.currentWorld || 'moon';
const ICON=n=>window.MoonshiftIcons?.markup(n)||'';
const names={moon:{name:'LUNA-7',place:'the Moon',tag:'LUNAR OUTPOST',icon:'🌙'},orbit:{name:'ORBITAL LAB',place:'Earth orbit',tag:'ORBITAL RESEARCH STATION',icon:'🛰'},mars:{name:'ARES-1',place:'Mars',tag:'MARTIAN OUTPOST',icon:'🔴'}};

// Each challenge is authored for a world rather than borrowing the Moon's copy or fictional equipment.

What will change its motion?',options:[['Wait for gravity to disappear','Orbit already has substantial gravity; waiting will not fix the drift.',0,4,false,'Orbit is free fall, not a place without gravity.'],['Push very gently the opposite way','A small force changes the ball’s speed and direction.',1,7,true,'Correct!
