 mars:{
  powerRouting:{title:'Dusty Solar Farm',icon:'solar',intro:'Mars receives less sunlight than Earth. Dust can reduce solar-panel output and drain the batteries.',problem:'Panels are dusty. How can you keep the outpost supplied?',options:[['Clean and inspect panels','Use time to restore the panels’ sunlight collection.',4,20,true,'Clean panels can produce more electricity in sunlight.'],['Run every machine harder','High demand drains the battery faster.',15,4,false,'More machines cannot create more sunlight.'],['Prioritize essential loads','Protect air and water while reducing optional power use.',2,8,true,'Essential systems are protected while the outpost saves energy.']]},
  greenhouseLight:{title:'Mars Green Dome',icon:'plant',intro:'Plants need light and water inside a protected habitat. Both resources are precious on Mars.',problem:'The crops need help. What should SPROUT try?',options:[['Balance growing lights and irrigation','Give plants enough of both without overusing the reserve.',4,12,true,'Healthy growing conditions require several resources together.'],['Turn on every grow light','High power use threatens the battery.',15,3,false,'Bright lights are not helpful if the power system fails.'],['Water only, no growing light','Water cannot replace the light plants use for photosynthesis.',1,8,false,'Plants need light as well as water.']]},
  co2Scrubber:{title:'Martian Habitat Air',icon:'air',intro:'The thin Martian atmosphere is not breathable. Crew rely on sealed rooms and air-processing equipment.',problem:'The CO₂ filter needs maintenance. What is the safest decision?',options:[['Inspect the filter and restore its cycle','Use some energy to keep the habitat air healthy.',4,15,true,'The habitat stays protected from CO₂ buildup.'],['Open the outside hatch for fresh air','Outside Mars air is too thin and mostly CO₂.',0,1,false,'Never open a habitat to the Martian atmosphere for breathing.'],['Change to a fresh filter cartridge','Use crew time and limited replacement supplies.',3,18,true,'A functioning filter is essential for crew safety.']]},
  waterRecovery:{title:'Mars Water Recovery Lab',icon:'water',intro:'Recovering and recycling water reduces the amount that a Mars outpost must carry or produce.',problem:'Recycled water needs a quality test. What should the crew do?',options:[['Treat and test the water','Spend energy to check purity before use.',4,15,true,'Good science: clean, then measure water quality.'],['Skip treatment to save energy','Unknown contaminants could make the water unsafe.',0,3,false,'Water must be verified safe before it is used.'],['Reuse a validated reserve','Use stored clean water while the recycler is repaired.',2,10,true,'A verified reserve is a useful backup for emergencies.']]},
  stormShutter:{title:'Mars Dust Storm Preparation',icon:'warning',intro:'Dust can coat equipment and reduce sunlight on Mars. A dust storm is not a solar radiation storm.',problem:'A dust storm is approaching. What is a smart first step?',options:[['Seal dust controls and check filters','Keep dust out of sensitive equipment.',5,20,true,'Dust controls protect the living area and machinery.'],['Open the exterior intake','This could let fine dust enter sensitive equipment.',0,2,false,'Protect the sealed habitat from airborne dust.'],['Conserve electricity and secure equipment','Reduce unnecessary use while protecting vulnerable machines.',3,12,true,'Resource planning matters during a long storm.']]},
  dsnAlign:{title:'Mars–Earth Relay',icon:'radio',intro:'Messages between Earth and Mars are delayed by minutes. The actual delay changes with planetary distance.',problem:'You must send a science update. Which method is most dependable?',options:[['Check and send a short verified packet','Use less energy and confirm the data are complete.',4,18,true,'A verified message is well suited to delayed communication.'],['Wait for an instant response','Radio signals cannot travel faster than light.',0,2,false,'An instant Earth reply is not possible from Mars.'],['Schedule a carefully checked transmission','Spend time checking the packet to reduce errors.',6,22,true,'Careful checks are valuable when every reply takes time.']]},
  gravityLab:{title:'Mars Gravity Experiment',icon:'satellite',intro:'Mars gravity is about 3.71 m/s², lower than Earth’s 9.81 m/s², but stronger than the Moon’s.',problem:'You release the same tool at the same height. How does it fall on Mars?',options:[['Slower than on Earth','Lower gravity gives a smaller downward acceleration.',0,8,true,'Correct: the tool falls more slowly than it would on Earth, ignoring air drag.'],['Exactly like on Earth','The gravitational accelerations are different.',0,4,false,'Gravity varies between planets.'],['It floats like in Earth orbit','Mars still has surface gravity.',0,4,false,'Objects fall down on the Martian surface.']]},
  roverSurvey:{title:'Rover Expedition Planner',icon:'satellite',intro:'A Mars rover must balance travel distance, limited energy, terrain difficulty, and science value.',problem:'Which route should the rover take today?',options:[['Short route with safe return','Fewer samples, more battery left for later.',4,10,true,'Good: the rover can return with a safety margin.'],['Long rocky shortcut with no reserve','More samples possible, but the rover could be stranded.',18,5,false,'Keep enough energy to return, not just to arrive.'],['Moderate route with a sample stop','Some science with a sensible energy reserve.',8,18,true,'Balanced exploration is an important mission skill.']]},
  thermalBalance:{title:'Mars Habitat Temperature',icon:'power',intro:'Mars gets very cold. Insulation and thermal systems help the habitat keep a comfortable temperature.',problem:'How should the team balance warmth and electricity?',options:[['Adjust heaters and insulate','A comfortable cabin without excessive electricity use.',5,16,true,'A combined thermal plan is more efficient.'],['Switch off every heater','Cold conditions could endanger systems and the crew.',0,2,false,'Saving power is not worth losing the safe cabin temperature.'],['Use maximum heaters all day','Wastes power the outpost may need for water and air.',14,3,false,'Efficient temperature control is better than always using maximum heat.']]}
 }
};

function applyOutcome(id,choice){
 const sim=window.Sim, w=world();
 const cost=choice[2],minutes=choice[3];
 // Resource reductions are real game-state changes, not merely decorative score popups.
 sim.batteryCharge=Math.max(0,sim.batteryCharge-cost);
 // Run a short fixed-duration resource simulation. Units: minutes -> seconds.
 sim.update(minutes*60);
 if(id==='co2Scrubber'){sim.scrubberOperational=true;sim.co2Level=Math.min(sim.co2Level,530);}
 if(id==='waterRecovery'){sim.recyclingEfficiency=Math.max(sim.recyclingEfficiency,.98);sim.waterPurityPercent=Math.max(sim.waterPurityPercent,99.4);}
 if(id==='stormShutter')sim.stormShelterShieldLocked=true;
 if(id==='dsnAlign'){sim.antennaAligned=true;sim.dsnLinkQuality=Math.max(sim.dsnLinkQuality,90);}
 if(id==='powerRouting'&&w==='mars')sim.solarEventMultiplier=Math.min(1,sim.solarEventMultiplier+.09);
 if(id==='greenhouseLight'&&w==='mars')sim.greenhouseCropHealth=Math.min(100,sim.greenhouseCropHealth+3);
 sim.update(0);sim.updateAlerts();
 window.Story?.decisions?.push({type:'worldChallenge',world:w,task:id,choice:choice[0],batteryCostKWh:cost,crewTimeMinutes:minutes,missionHour:sim.missionTimeHours});
 window.Save?.schedule?.();
}

function mount(){
 if(!window.Minigames||!window.MissionUI)return;
 const ui=window.MissionUI, games=window.Minigames;
 if(ui._v21Installed)return;
 ui._v21Installed=true;
 const origModal=ui.modal.bind(ui);
 ui.modal=function(title,content){
  const profile=info();
  const cleanTitle=world()!=='moon'?title.replace(/LUNA-7|THE MOON|LUNAR(?!\s+GRAVITY)/gi, profile.name):title;
  const overlay=origModal(cleanTitle,content);
  const subtitle=overlay.querySelector('.task-header .task-subtitle');
  if(subtitle)subtitle.textContent=`${profile.name} · ${profile.tag} · TRAINING GAME`;
  const icon=overlay.querySelector('.task-header .task-icon');
  if(icon)icon.innerHTML=world()==='moon'?'🌙':(world()==='orbit'?ICON('satellite'):ICON('solar'));
  return overlay;
 };
 const origHelp=ui.showHelp.bind(ui);
 ui.showHelp=function(){origHelp();if(world()==='moon')return;const root=document.querySelector('.mission-guide-overlay');if(!root)return;const lead=root.querySelector('.guide-lead');if(lead)lead.textContent=`You're an astronaut exploring ${info().place}. Find your next glowing console, try a science experiment, and keep your crew safe.`;root.querySelectorAll('.science-note').forEach(e=>{e.textContent='The science ideas are real, but the missions and resource amounts are simplified training examples, not measurements from NASA hardware.'});};
 const origMenu=ui.showMainMenu.bind(ui);
 ui.showMainMenu=function(){origMenu();if(world()==='moon')return;const root=document.querySelector('.mission-guide-overlay');const lead=root?.querySelector('.guide-lead');if(lead)lead.textContent=`Your crew tablet for ${info().name}. Your journey and settings stay available here.`;};
 const origScience=ui.showScienceSources.bind(ui);
 ui.showScienceSources=function(){origScience();if(world()==='moon')return;const root=document.querySelector('.mission-guide-overlay');if(!root)return;const lead=root.querySelector('.guide-lead');if(lead)lead.textContent=`${info().name} is a fictional science adventure. Gravity, signal delay, and environmental trade-offs use real principles; resource values are training examples.`;};
 const origActivities=ui.showActivities.bind(ui);
 ui.showActivities=function(){
  if(world()==='moon')return origActivities();
  const w=world();
  const picks=w==='orbit'?[
   ['task_gravity','Free-Float Physics','In a station in orbit, gravity has not disappeared.'],
   ['task_rover','Cargo Transfer','Move objects carefully through the laboratory.'],
   ['task_thermal','Thermal Control','Balance heating and electrical supply.']
  ]:[
   ['task_gravity','Mars Gravity','See how motion changes on another planet.'],
   ['task_rover','Rover Planner','Choose a safe route across the surface.'],
   ['task_thermal','Habitat Heating','Keep warm while saving electricity.']
  ];
  const m=ui.modal(`EXPLORE ${info().name}`,`<p class="guide-lead">Three optional activities in ${info().place}. Choose one to find its station on your original map.</p><div class="activity-list">${picks.map(([id,t,d])=>`<button class="activity-choice" data-v21-destination="${id}"><span class="activity-icon">${ICON('satellite')}</span><span class="activity-copy"><strong>${t}</strong><small>${d}</small></span><span class="activity-go">FIND ↗</span></button>`).join('')}</div>`);
  m.querySelectorAll('[data-v21-destination]').forEach(btn=>btn.onclick=()=>{
   const item=window.StationMap?.interactables?.find(t=>t.id===btn.dataset.v21Destination);
   if(!item)return;
   ui.waypoint={x:item.x,y:item.y,roomId:item.roomId,label:item.shortLabel||item.name,act:window.Story.currentAct};
   m.remove();ui.notify(`Follow the gold arrow to ${item.shortLabel||item.name}.`);
  });
 };
 const oldGuide=games.getGuideData.bind(games);
 games.getGuideData=function(k){const m=/^(orbit|mars)_(.+)$/.exec(k||'');if(!m)return oldGuide(k);const task=TASKS[m[1]][m[2]];if(!task)return oldGuide(k);return {title:task.title,summary:task.intro,what:task.problem,normal:'One of the plans gives a safe result without wasting resources.',low:'Not enough power, time, or preparation can put the mission at risk.',high:'Too much energy or a sudden force can cause new problems.',tip:'Compare the consequences. It is okay to try again.',steps:['Read the problem','Compare three plans','Choose and see what would happen','Try again if the plan is unsafe']};};
 const oldStart=games.startTask.bind(games);
 games.startTask=function(id,onComplete){
   const w=world(), data=TASKS[w]?.[id];
   if(!data)return oldStart(id,onComplete);
   if(window.Sim?.missionFailed){window.PowerCrisis?.show?.();return;}
   this.onComplete=onComplete;this.currentTaskId=`${w}_${id}`;
   const modal=this.createModal(data.title,`${info().name} · ${data.intro}`,'✦');
   modal.classList.add('v21-world-task');
   const body=modal.querySelector('#taskBody'),hint=modal.querySelector('#taskHint'),action=modal.querySelector('#taskActionBtn');
   let selected=-1;let confirmed=false;
   body.innerHTML=`<div class="v21-science-panel"><div class="v21-science-picture">${ICON(data.icon)}</div><div><span class="v21-overline">EXPLORE · LEARN · DECIDE</span><h3>${data.title}</h3><p>${data.intro}</p></div></div><div class="v21-task-question"><strong>${data.problem}</strong><p>Choose a plan, then see whether it solves the problem.</p></div><div class="v21-choices">${data.options.map((o,i)=>`<button type="button" data-i="${i}" class="v21-choice"><b>${String.fromCharCode(65+i)}</b><span><strong>${o[0]}</strong><small>${o[1]}</small></span><span class="v21-choice-end">${o[2]} kWh · ${o[3]} min</span></button>`).join('')}</div><div class="v21-feedback" role="status" aria-live="polite" hidden></div><p class="v21-fiction-note">Educational scenario: costs and crew times are illustrative, not NASA measurements. Unsafe choices do not consume resources; retry freely.</p>`;
   const buttons=[...body.querySelectorAll('.v21-choice')];
   buttons.forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{
    if(confirmed)return;
    selected=Number(b.dataset.i);const o=data.options[selected];
    buttons.forEach(e=>e.classList.toggle('is-selected',e===b));
    const notice=body.querySelector('.v21-feedback');notice.hidden=false;notice.classList.toggle('is-success',o[4]);
    notice.innerHTML=`<strong>${o[4]?'Good engineering plan':'Let’s try another plan'}</strong><span>${o[5]}</span>`;
    const available=window.Sim?.batteryCharge||0;
    action.disabled=!o[4]||available<o[2];
    action.textContent=!o[4]?'CHOOSE A SAFER PLAN':available<o[2]?'NOT ENOUGH STORED ENERGY':'CONFIRM THIS PLAN';
    hint.textContent=!o[4]?'Your idea is worth testing. Try one of the safe options.':available<o[2]?'This plan costs more energy than your battery holds. Choose a lower-energy option.':`This plan costs ${o[2]} kWh and about ${o[3]} minutes of crew time.`;
   });
   action.textContent='SELECT A PLAN';action.disabled=true;
   hint.textContent='Pick one of the three plans. You can change your mind before confirming.';
   action.onclick=()=>{
    if(selected<0||confirmed)return;
    const choice=data.options[selected];
    if(!choice[4]||window.Sim.batteryCharge<choice[2])return;
    confirmed=true;applyOutcome(id,choice);
    this.close();onComplete?.(id);
    window.Audio?.taskSuccess?.();
    ui.notify(`${data.title}: ${choice[0]} · Battery −${choice[2]} kWh.`);
   };
 };
 // When switching worlds, remove stale failure state from the previous world's training scenario.
 const originalSwitch=window.Beyond?.activateWorld?.bind(window.Beyond);
 if(originalSwitch)window.Beyond.activateWorld=function(id){const old=world();if(id!==old){document.querySelectorAll('.v20-failure-overlay').forEach(x=>x.remove());window.Sim.missionFailed=false;}return originalSwitch(id);};
 // Ensure status is honest on load without claiming a failure when sunlight can power every machine.
 const oldRest=window.Save?.restore?.bind(window.Save);
 if(oldRest)window.Save.restore=function(){const ok=oldRest();if(ok){window.Sim.missionFailed=false;window.Sim.update(0);}return ok;};
 // Correct the persistent UI title when returning from a map or world switch.
 const origUpdate=ui.update.bind(ui);
 ui.update=function(){origUpdate();const title=document.querySelector('.hud-room-badge');const w=info();if(title&&title.textContent.startsWith('LUNA-7')&&world()!=='moon')title.textContent=title.textContent.replace(/^LUNA-7/,w.name);};
 window.MoonshiftWorldTasks=TASKS;
}
if(document.readyState==='loading')window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',mount);
else mount();
})();

</script>

const keyFor=(w,id)=>`moonshift.v23.best.${w}.${id}`;
const modeKey='moonshift.v23.mode';

const getMode=()=>{try{return localStorage.getItem(modeKey)==='specialist'?'specialist':'explorer'}catch(e){return'explorer'}};
const svg=(html)=>`<svg class="v23-graphic" viewBox="0 0 520 300" role="img" aria-label="Interactive science demonstration" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"><defs><linearGradient id="v23-bg" x1="0" y1="0" x2="1" y2="1"><stop stop-color="#142e40"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="#091b29"/></linearGradient><linearGradient id="v23-h2o"><stop stop-color="#72d2e9"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="#269bb6"/></linearGradient></defs><rect width="520" height="300" rx="22" fill="url(#v23-bg)"/>${html}</svg>`;

};
function createState(kind){
 const sim=window.Sim;
 switch(kind){
 case'power':return{loads:{air:12,lights:2,radio:4,science:6},moves:0};
 case'garden':return{light:5,water:2,moves:0};
 case'shield':return{locks:[false,false,false],manual:false,moves:0};
 case'drift':return{x:90,v:1.5,moves:0};
 case'docking':return{distance:90,speed:4,offset:23,moves:0};
 case'air':return{fan:false,filter:false,oxygen:false,moves:0};
 case'water':return{stage:0,wrong:0,moves:0};
 case'solar':return{clean:[false,false,false,false,false,false],economy:true,moves:0};
 case'route':return{route:null,driving:false,travel:0,moves:0};
 case'signal':return{angle:65,packet:'compressed',sent:false,travel:0,moves:0};
 case'thermal':return{temp:16,moves:0};
 case'gravity':return{worldChoice:'earth',dropped:new Set(),moves:0,travel:0};
 default:return{moves:0}
 }
}
function renderScene(lab){
 const {kind,state:s,w}=lab;
 const titleColor=w==='mars'?'#f7b889':'#87d8ec';
 switch(kind){
 case'power':return circuit(s);
 case'garden':{
  const healthy=s.light>=7&&s.water>=2,leaf=healthy?'#88cf95':'#b9ad70',h=healthy?96:62;
  return svg(`<circle cx="400" cy="72" r="39" fill="#f7d69b" opacity="${.3+.7*(s.light/15)}"/><path d="M400 8V26M400 118V138M348 72H330M452 72H470" stroke="#e4bd79" stroke-width="4"/><rect x="65" y="220" width="390" height="45" rx="14" fill="#344f53"/><rect x="210" y="189" width="110" height="61" rx="12" fill="#af7864"/><path d="M264 198V${198-h}" stroke="#90c894" stroke-width="8" stroke-linecap="round"/><ellipse cx="239" cy="${198-h+24}" rx="36" ry="19" fill="${leaf}" transform="rotate(26 239 ${198-h+24})"/><ellipse cx="290" cy="${198-h+7}" rx="35" ry="17" fill="${leaf}" transform="rotate(-28 290 ${198-h+7})"/><path d="M160 95C205 74 221 115 245 119" stroke="url(#v23-h2o)" stroke-width="${3+s.water*2}" stroke-linecap="round" fill="none"/><path d="M154 69L170 69L170 95L154 95Z" fill="#80c2d5"/><text x="90" y="42" fill="#d9edf1" font-size="16">Light ${s.light} kW</text><text x="90" y="64" fill="#a7cedb" font-size="14">Water ${s.water} L/h</text><text x="350" y="205" fill="${leaf}" font-size="17">${healthy?'Growing':'Slower growth'}</text>`);
 }
 case'shield':return svg(`<rect x="68" y="37" width="384" height="220" rx="25" fill="#233d51" stroke="#7297a6" stroke-width="5"/><rect x="148" y="61" width="224" height="170" rx="70" fill="#142c3b" stroke="#769bad" stroke-width="7"/>${s.locks.map((on,i)=>`<rect x="${84+i*130}" y="${on?64:30}" width="103" height="${on?155:44}" rx="13" fill="${on?'#7fb3b0':'#58758b'}" opacity="${on?.87:.52}" stroke="${on?'#9ee0bf':'#b7d6e1'}" stroke-width="3"/>`).join('')}<text x="260" y="275" text-anchor="middle" fill="${s.locks.every(Boolean)?'#a4efbb':'#e2c7a3'}" font-size="16">${s.locks.filter(Boolean).length} / 3 protective locks secured</text>`);
 case'drift':return svg(`<path d="M32 96H482M32 224H482" stroke="#456475" stroke-width="4"/><rect x="375" y="98" width="76" height="124" rx="16" fill="rgba(113,194,208,.13)" stroke="#96d8d8" stroke-dasharray="7 9" stroke-width="4"/><text x="410" y="81" fill="#ade7e9" text-anchor="middle" font-size="15">Catch zone</text><rect x="${s.x}" y="142" width="45" height="45" rx="12" fill="#b4cbd6" stroke="#f5fcff" stroke-width="3"/><path d="M${s.x+10} 154L${s.x+34} 177M${s.x+34} 154L${s.x+10} 177" stroke="#36566a" stroke-width="3"/><path d="M${s.x+45} 165h${Math.min(90,Math.max(10,s.v*25))}" stroke="${titleColor}" stroke-width="4" marker-end="none"/><text x="260" y="269" fill="#eaf6fb" text-anchor="middle" font-size="16">Drift speed ${fmt(s.v,1)} units/s · Try to stop inside the zone</text>`);
 case'docking':return svg(`<rect x="375" y="91" width="125" height="126" rx="18" fill="#2d5063" stroke="#a6d8de" stroke-width="5"/><rect x="390" y="122" width="63" height="63" rx="10" fill="#142e3e" stroke="#a1cfcf" stroke-width="4"/><text x="415" y="72" fill="#e1f6fd" font-size="16" text-anchor="middle">Dock</text><g transform="translate(${U.clamp(355-s.distance*3,55,355)} ${150+s.offset})"><path d="M-28 -28L28 0L-28 28L-17 0Z" fill="#b5dfea" stroke="#d8f7ff" stroke-width="4"/></g><path d="M80 242H445" stroke="#456477" stroke-dasharray="6 12"/><text x="255" y="272" fill="#e7f7fa" font-size="17" text-anchor="middle">${fmt(s.distance)} m away · ${fmt(s.speed,1)} m/s closing · alignment ${fmt(s.offset)} m</text>`);
 case'air':return svg(`<path d="M58 144H460" stroke="#486d83" stroke-width="22" stroke-linecap="round"/><path d="M58 144H${s.fan?445:95}" stroke="#75c5dd" stroke-width="12" stroke-dasharray="15 12"/><circle cx="115" cy="144" r="46" fill="${s.fan?'#2a7488':'#31495b'}" stroke="#8bd8e7" stroke-width="4"/><path d="M115 111V178M83 144H148" stroke="#c0e6ee" stroke-width="8"/><rect x="210" y="96" width="100" height="96" rx="15" fill="${s.filter?'#417c79':'#3a4b57'}" stroke="#83cfbd" stroke-width="4"/><path d="M225 126H296M225 147H296M225 168H296" stroke="#e3f3ee" stroke-width="5"/><circle cx="416" cy="143" r="45" fill="${s.oxygen?'#2a7784':'#344a58'}" stroke="#8ed8e8" stroke-width="4"/><text x="416" y="152" fill="#eef8fb" font-weight="700" font-size="21" text-anchor="middle">O₂</text><text x="110" y="215" fill="#daecf5" text-anchor="middle" font-size="16">Fan</text><text x="260" y="215" fill="#daecf5" text-anchor="middle" font-size="16">CO₂ filter</text><text x="420" y="215" fill="#daecf5" text-anchor="middle" font-size="16">Oxygen</text><text x="260" y="42" fill="#d0edf1" text-anchor="middle" font-size="17">Air recycling loop</text>`);
 case'water':return svg(`<path d="M65 148H454" stroke="#324c5f" stroke-width="23" stroke-linecap="round"/><path d="M65 148H${65+Math.min(390,(s.stage/4)*390)}" stroke="#6ad2ee" stroke-width="11" stroke-dasharray="11 8" stroke-linecap="round"/>${['Collect','Filter','Check','Store'].map((t,i)=>`<circle cx="${83+i*115}" cy="148" r="35" fill="${s.stage>i?'#276a79':'#274156'}" stroke="${s.stage>i?'#9fe7d6':'#82b6cf'}" stroke-width="4"/><text x="${83+i*115}" y="155" fill="#f0f8fd" font-size="19" font-weight="700" text-anchor="middle">${s.stage>i?'✓':i+1}</text><text x="${83+i*115}" y="222" fill="#dbeef4" font-size="17" text-anchor="middle">${t}</text>`).join('')}<text x="260" y="50" fill="#cce8f5" text-anchor="middle" font-size="18">Water recovery pipeline</text>`);
 case'solar':return svg(`<text x="260" y="43" fill="#dbeefa" font-size="18" text-anchor="middle">Brush off the dust to restore sunlight</text>${s.clean.map((on,i)=>{const x=90+(i%3)*120,y=83+Math.floor(i/3)*82;return `<g data-panel="${i}"><rect x="${x}" y="${y}" width="100" height="64" rx="8" fill="${on?'#227d9c':'#7d665e'}" stroke="${on?'#84d8ec':'#d3ac8c'}" stroke-width="3"/><path d="M${x+12} ${y+15}h76M${x+12} ${y+32}h76M${x+12} ${y+49}h76" stroke="${on?'#8ed3ec':'#a38872'}" stroke-width="3"/><text x="${x+50}" y="${y+40}" text-anchor="middle" fill="#fcf9f3" font-size="15">${on?'✓':'Dust'}</text></g>`}).join('')}<text x="260" y="281" fill="#f2dbb5" text-anchor="middle" font-size="17">Panels cleaned: ${s.clean.filter(Boolean).length}/6</text>`);
 case'route':{
  const inOrbit=lab.w==='orbit';
  const veh=inOrbit?`<rect x="-23" y="-17" width="46" height="34" rx="7" fill="#a4cad5" stroke="#e2f4f6" stroke-width="3"/><path d="M-14 -9L14 9M14 -9L-14 9" stroke="#648fa3" stroke-width="3"/>`:`<rect x="-22" y="-15" width="44" height="28" rx="8" fill="#b9cbd3"/><circle cx="-13" cy="17" r="7" fill="#233d4c"/><circle cx="13" cy="17" r="7" fill="#233d4c"/><rect x="-9" y="-24" width="18" height="14" rx="5" fill="#8fd8ef"/>`;
  return svg(`<path d="M73 236Q260 185 447 86" stroke="#a1bdd0" stroke-width="11" fill="none" opacity=".25"/><path d="M73 236Q260 ${s.route==='risky'?15:s.route==='science'?175:255} 447 86" stroke="${s.route?'#e6c28b':'#627f8a'}" stroke-width="5" stroke-dasharray="9 9" fill="none"/><circle cx="73" cy="236" r="23" fill="#345967" stroke="#b8e0e1" stroke-width="4"/><circle cx="447" cy="86" r="23" fill="#345967" stroke="#b8e0e1" stroke-width="4"/><text x="73" y="278" fill="#d5f1f5" text-anchor="middle" font-size="15">${inOrbit?'Cargo':'Base'}</text><text x="447" y="48" fill="#d5f1f5" text-anchor="middle" font-size="15">${inOrbit?'Module':'Research'}</text><g transform="translate(${73+s.travel*374} ${(1-s.travel)*(1-s.travel)*236+2*(1-s.travel)*s.travel*(s.route==='risky'?15:s.route==='science'?175:255)+s.travel*s.travel*86})">${veh}</g><text x="262" y="281" text-anchor="middle" fill="#f4dcba" font-size="17">${s.route?s.route==='safe'?'Safe route':s.route==='science'?'Science route':'Risky shortcut':'Choose a route'}</text>`);
 }
 case'signal':return svg(`<circle cx="122" cy="142" r="56" fill="#243e51" stroke="#9fd6e4" stroke-width="4"/><circle cx="122" cy="142" r="35" fill="#327698"/><path d="M122 142l${Math.cos((s.angle-90)*Math.PI/180)*85} ${Math.sin((s.angle-90)*Math.PI/180)*85}" stroke="#eac390" stroke-width="8" stroke-linecap="round"/><path d="M122 142l${Math.cos((116-90)*Math.PI/180)*90} ${Math.sin((116-90)*Math.PI/180)*90}" stroke="#8cdcd5" stroke-width="3" stroke-dasharray="6 7"/><circle cx="419" cy="140" r="49" fill="#286aa8" stroke="#b2d5e8" stroke-width="4"/><path d="M402 108Q430 108 428 125Q423 142 444 156L427 184L400 160L405 139Z" fill="#8ec89d"/><path d="M203 140H370" stroke="#89c9de" stroke-width="3" stroke-dasharray="6 7"/><circle cx="${203+s.travel*167}" cy="140" r="8" fill="#fae6a1"/><text x="262" y="244" fill="#d8edf4" font-size="17" text-anchor="middle">Antenna ${s.angle}° · target 116°</text>`);
 case'thermal':return svg(`<rect x="198" y="35" width="124" height="203" rx="22" fill="#23465c" stroke="#8cc8e0" stroke-width="4"/><rect x="245" y="70" width="30" height="137" rx="15" fill="#153141"/><rect x="245" y="${207-(s.temp/30)*137}" width="30" height="${s.temp/30*137}" rx="13" fill="${s.temp>=19&&s.temp<=23?'#8adeb9':'#f1b880'}"/><circle cx="260" cy="209" r="24" fill="${s.temp>=19&&s.temp<=23?'#8adeb9':'#f1b880'}"/><text x="260" y="277" text-anchor="middle" font-size="22" font-weight="700" fill="#e8f6fb">${s.temp}°C</text><text x="84" y="142" font-size="17" fill="#b6d3e1">Too cold</text><text x="362" y="142" font-size="17" fill="#b6d3e1">Too hot</text>`);
 case'gravity':{
 const g={earth:9.81,moon:1.62,mars:3.71};const names={earth:'Earth',moon:'Moon',mars:'Mars'};
 return svg(`${Object.entries(g).map(([key,val],i)=>{const x=78+i*148,t=Math.sqrt(2*4/val);const y=s.dropped.has(key)?(key===s.worldChoice?90+133*(s.travel||0):223):90;return `<rect x="${x-32}" y="54" width="64" height="170" rx="12" fill="rgba(116,156,174,.12)" stroke="${key===s.worldChoice?'#f1d69e':'#577b8a'}" stroke-width="3"/><circle cx="${x}" cy="${y}" r="15" fill="${key==='mars'?'#df967d':key==='moon'?'#d8e0e6':'#67b8e6'}"/><text x="${x}" y="248" text-anchor="middle" font-size="15" fill="#edf6f7">${names[key]}</text><text x="${x}" y="272" text-anchor="middle" font-size="14" fill="#b9dae5">${fmt(t,2)} s</text>`}).join('')}<text x="260" y="30" text-anchor="middle" font-size="17" fill="#def3f8">Drop from the same 4 m height</text>`)
 }
 default:return svg('');
 }
}
function feedback(lab){
 const s=lab.state,mode=lab.mode,kind=lab.kind,sim=window.Sim;
 let ok=false,why='',cost=2,updates=()=>{},points=65;
 switch(kind){
 case'power':{const t=Object.values(s.loads).reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)+(sim.powerAllocations.thermalHeaters||3);ok=s.moves>0&&s.loads.air>=10&&s.loads.radio>=2&&t<=sim.solarGeneration+4;why=`Demand ${fmt(t,1)} kW; sunlight ${fmt(sim.solarGeneration,1)} kW. ${ok?'Essential machines stay supplied.':'Give air at least 10 kW, radio at least 2 kW, and trim optional loads.'}`;cost=0;points=U.clamp(Math.round(105-Math.max(0,t-sim.solarGeneration)*6),55,100);updates=()=>{sim.setPowerAllocation('lifeSupport',s.loads.air);sim.setPowerAllocation('greenhouse',s.loads.lights);sim.setPowerAllocation('communications',s.loads.radio);sim.setPowerAllocation('auxiliary',s.loads.science);};break;}
 case'garden':{const current=sim.powerAllocations.greenhouse,net=sim.solarGeneration-(sim.totalDemand-current+s.light);ok=s.light>=7&&s.light<=10&&s.water>=2&&s.water<=3;why=`Grow lights ${s.light} kW (target 7–10); irrigation ${s.water} L/h (target 2–3). ${ok?'Excellent: a balanced greenhouse recipe.':'Move both sliders into their highlighted target ranges.'} ${net<0?'Station battery is currently supplying '+fmt(-net,1)+' kW; that station-wide reading does not invalidate your plant experiment.':'Station has '+fmt(net,1)+' kW power surplus.'}`;cost=0;updates=()=>{sim.setPowerAllocation('greenhouse',s.light);sim.gardenWaterLitresPerHour=s.water;sim.greenhouseCropHealth=U.clamp(sim.greenhouseCropHealth+2,0,100);};break;}
 case'shield':ok=s.locks.every(Boolean);cost=s.manual?1:6;why=ok?'All three protective mechanisms are secured. The manual option costs crew time; powered shutters cost stored electricity.':'Secure all three shutters before choosing a method.';updates=()=>{sim.stormShelterShieldLocked=true;};break;
 case'drift':ok=Math.abs(s.x-385)<37&&Math.abs(s.v)<1.6;why=ok?`Perfect catch! The kit reached the rail gently (${fmt(s.v,1)} motion units/s).`:'Move the cargo into the striped catch area, then slow its drift with a gentle opposing push.';cost=1;break;
 case'docking':ok=s.distance<=20&&s.speed<=2.6&&Math.abs(s.offset)<=12;why=ok?'Soft capture! Relative movement and alignment are both safe.':'Aim for distance ≤ 20 m, speed ≤ 2.6 m/s, and alignment within 12 m (training tolerance).';cost=3;break;
 case'air':ok=s.fan&&s.filter&&s.oxygen;why=ok?'Air circulates, CO₂ is removed, and oxygen management is online.':'Turn on the fan, CO₂ filter, and oxygen system. All three matter.';cost=3;updates=()=>{sim.scrubberOperational=true;sim.oxygenGeneratorOperational=true;sim.co2Level=Math.min(530,sim.co2Level);};break;
 case'water':ok=s.stage>=4;why=ok?'Water is collected, cleaned, checked and stored in the correct sequence.':'Tap the treatment stages in order: collect → filter → check → store.';cost=2;updates=()=>{sim.recyclingEfficiency=Math.max(.98,sim.recyclingEfficiency);sim.waterPurityPercent=Math.max(99.4,sim.waterPurityPercent);};break;
 case'solar':{const cleaned=s.clean.filter(Boolean).length;ok=cleaned>=4&&s.economy;why=ok?`${cleaned}/6 panels cleaned. Reduced optional loads protect the battery while solar generation improves.`:'Clean at least 4 solar panels and switch on energy-saving mode.';cost=4;updates=()=>{sim.solarEventMultiplier=Math.min(1,sim.solarEventMultiplier+.18);sim.setPowerAllocation('auxiliary',Math.min(2,sim.powerAllocations.auxiliary));};points=Math.round(70+cleaned*4);break;}
 case'route':ok=s.travel>=1&&['safe','science'].includes(s.route);why=ok?(lab.w==='orbit'?'Cargo arrived safely at the next research module.':'Rover returned with a battery reserve and scientific observations.'):s.route==='risky'?'A risky shortcut has no safe reserve. Try the safer or science route.':'Choose a route and drive the rover to the marked destination.';cost=s.route==='science'?7:4;break;
 case'signal':{const aligned=Math.abs(s.angle-116)<=12;ok=aligned&&s.sent&&s.travel>=1;why=ok?`The packet was transmitted using the aligned antenna. ${lab.w==='mars'?'One-way light-time ≈ 12.5 minutes at this example distance.':'Low Earth orbit allows much shorter light-time.'}`:'Aim at 116° (acceptable range 104–128°), select a packet and send it.';cost=s.packet==='compressed'?3:6;updates=()=>{sim.antennaAligned=true;sim.dsnLinkQuality=Math.max(90,sim.dsnLinkQuality);};break;}
 case'thermal':ok=s.temp>=19&&s.temp<=23;why=ok?`Cabin stays at ${s.temp}°C using a controlled setting.`:'Aim between 19°C and 23°C in this fictional mission.';cost=3;updates=()=>{sim.temperatureC=s.temp;sim.setPowerAllocation('thermalHeaters',s.temp<=20?4:6);};break;
 case'gravity':ok=s.dropped.has('earth')&&s.dropped.has(lab.w==='mars'?'mars':'moon');why=ok?'Nice experiment: the same object falls at different speeds under different gravitational accelerations.':'Run at least two drops: Earth and this mission’s world.';cost=0;break;
 }
 if(mode==='explorer')points=Math.max(points,70);
 return{ok,why,cost,updates,points};
}
function controls(lab){
 const s=lab.state,kind=lab.kind,mode=lab.mode;
 const action=(a,label)=>`<button type="button" class="v23-ctrl" data-op="${a}">${label}</button>`;
 switch(kind){
 case'power':return`<p class="v23-controls-note">Tap a system to cycle its power. Air and communication come first.</p><div class="v23-grid-controls">${Object.entries(s.loads).map(([k,v])=>action('load-'+k,`${{air:'💨 Air',lights:'🌱 Plants',radio:'📡 Radio',science:'🔬 Science'}[k]}<b>${v} kW</b>`)).join('')}</div>`;
 case'garden':return`<div class="v30-target-header">🌱 Plant recipe <strong>${s.light>=7&&s.light<=10&&s.water>=2&&s.water<=3?'✓ Ready to grow':'Adjust two values'}</strong></div><label class="v23-range-label">Grow lights <b>${s.light} kW</b><input data-op="light" type="range" min="2" max="12" value="${s.light}" style="--optimal-from:50%;--optimal-to:80%"/><small class="v30-optimum">✓ Recommended: 7–10 kW · ${s.light>=7&&s.light<=10?'IN RANGE':'OUTSIDE RANGE'}</small></label><label class="v23-range-label">Irrigation <b>${s.water} L/hour</b><input data-op="water" type="range" min="1" max="5" value="${s.water}" style="--optimal-from:25%;--optimal-to:50%"/><small class="v30-optimum">✓ Recommended: 2–3 L/hour · ${s.water>=2&&s.water<=3?'IN RANGE':'OUTSIDE RANGE'}</small></label><div class="v30-science-disclaimer">Training model values, not real crop requirements on Mars. These targets are specific to this activity.</div>`;
 case'shield':return`<p class="v23-controls-note">Tap all three shutters, then pick an actuation method.</p><div class="v23-grid-controls">${[0,1,2].map(i=>action('lock-'+i,`Shield ${i+1} ${s.locks[i]?'✓':'○'}`)).join('')}</div><div class="v23-grid-controls">${action('manual',s.manual?'✓ Manual · 1 kWh':'Manual · 1 kWh')}${action('powered',!s.manual?'✓ Powered · 6 kWh':'Powered · 6 kWh')}</div>`;
 case'drift':return`<p class="v23-controls-note">Push gently, allow one second of drift, then brake near the catch rail.</p><div class="v23-grid-controls">${action('push-left','← Gentle left')}${action('push-right','Gentle right →')}${action('drift-step','▶ Drift 1 second')}${action('drift-reset','↻ Restart motion')}</div>`;
 case'docking':return`<p class="v23-controls-note">Target: distance ≤20 m, speed ≤2.6 m/s, alignment ±12 m. Brake first, then align and approach.</p><div class="v23-grid-controls">${action('brake','Brake −0.6 m/s')}${action('approach','▶ Approach')}${action('offset-up','↑ Align upward')}${action('offset-down','↓ Align downward')}</div>`;
 case'air':return`<p class="v23-controls-note">Switch on each stage of the closed air circuit.</p><div class="v23-grid-controls">${action('fan',`${s.fan?'✓':'○'} Circulation fan`)}${action('filter',`${s.filter?'✓':'○'} CO₂ filter`)}${action('oxygen',`${s.oxygen?'✓':'○'} Oxygen support`)}</div>`;
 case'water':return`<p class="v23-controls-note">Build a clean-water route in the correct order. Tap each step.</p><div class="v23-grid-controls">${['Collect','Filter','Check quality','Store'].map((t,i)=>action('water-'+i,`${s.stage>i?'✓':'○'} ${t}`)).join('')}</div>`;
 case'solar':return`<p class="v23-controls-note">Clean the panels directly in the illustration, then manage power use.</p><div class="v23-grid-controls">${action('economy',s.economy?'✓ Save optional power':'○ Save optional power')}${action('solar-all','🧽 Brush one dirty panel')}</div>`;
 case'route':return`<p class="v23-controls-note">Pick your route and watch the rover travel through the map.</p><div class="v23-grid-controls">${action('route-safe',`${s.route==='safe'?'✓ ':''}Safe · 4 kWh`)}${action('route-science',`${s.route==='science'?'✓ ':''}Science · 7 kWh`)}${action('route-risky',`${s.route==='risky'?'✓ ':''}Rocky shortcut · risky`)}${action('run-route',lab.w==='orbit'?'▶ Move cargo':'▶ Drive rover')}</div>`;
 case'signal':return`<div class="v30-target-header">📡 Target 116° · safe 104–128°</div><label class="v23-range-label">Antenna pointing <b>${s.angle}°</b><input data-op="angle" type="range" min="65" max="160" step="1" value="${s.angle}"/></label><div class="v23-grid-controls">${action('packet',s.packet==='compressed'?'✓ Compact science packet':'Full data packet')}${action('send','📡 Send the message')}</div>`;
 case'thermal':return`<p class="v23-controls-note">Cabin target: 19–23°C. Current: ${s.temp}°C. ${s.temp>=19&&s.temp<=23?'✓ Comfortable':'Adjust to highlighted safe range.'}</p><div class="v23-grid-controls">${action('temp-down','− Cool 1°C')}${action('temp-up','+ Heat 1°C')}</div>`;
 case'gravity':return`<p class="v23-controls-note">Select a world, release a ball, and compare the illustrated fall times.</p><div class="v23-grid-controls">${['earth','moon','mars'].map(w=>action('gravity-'+w,`${s.worldChoice===w?'✓ ':''}${w[0].toUpperCase()+w.slice(1)}`)).join('')}${action('drop','↓ Drop ball')}</div>`;
 }
 return '';
}
function runOp(lab,op,value){
 const s=lab.state,kind=lab.kind;
 if(op.startsWith('load-')){const k=op.slice(5);const opts={air:[8,10,12,15],lights:[1,2,5,8],radio:[0,2,4,6],science:[0,2,4,8]}[k];if(opts){const i=opts.indexOf(s.loads[k]);s.loads[k]=opts[(i+1)%opts.length];}}
 else if(op==='light'||op==='water')s[op]=+value;
 else if(op.startsWith('lock-'))s.locks[+op.slice(5)]=!s.locks[+op.slice(5)];
 else if(op==='manual')s.manual=true;
 else if(op==='powered')s.manual=false;
 else if(op==='push-left')s.v=U.clamp(s.v-.6,-3,3);
 else if(op==='push-right')s.v=U.clamp(s.v+.6,-3,3);
 else if(op==='drift-step')s.x=U.clamp(s.x+s.v*18,65,430);
 else if(op==='drift-reset'){s.x=90;s.v=1.5;}
 else if(op==='brake')s.speed=U.clamp(s.speed-.6,.5,6);
 else if(op==='approach')s.distance=U.clamp(s.distance-s.speed*8,0,90);
 else if(op==='offset-up')s.offset=U.clamp(s.offset-8,-38,38);
 else if(op==='offset-down')s.offset=U.clamp(s.offset+8,-38,38);
 else if(['fan','filter','oxygen'].includes(op))s[op]=!s[op];
 else if(op.startsWith('water-')){const i=+op.slice(6);if(i===s.stage)s.stage++;else s.wrong++;}
 else if(op==='economy')s.economy=!s.economy;
 else if(op==='solar-all'){const i=s.clean.findIndex(x=>!x);if(i!==-1)s.clean[i]=true;}
 else if(op.startsWith('solar-panel-'))s.clean[+op.slice(12)]=true;
 else if(op.startsWith('route-')){s.route=op.slice(6);s.travel=0;s.driving=false;}
 else if(op==='run-route'){if(s.route){s.driving=true;s.travel=0;lab.animate('travel',1.45);}}
 else if(op==='angle')s.angle=+value;
 else if(op==='packet')s.packet=s.packet==='compressed'?'full':'compressed';
 else if(op==='send'){
  if(Math.abs(s.angle-116)<=9){s.sent=true;s.travel=0;lab.animate('travel',1.65);}
  else lab.message('Align the dish with the target first — aim near 116°.','warning');
 }
 else if(op==='temp-up')s.temp=U.clamp(s.temp+1,10,30);
 else if(op==='temp-down')s.temp=U.clamp(s.temp-1,10,30);
 else if(op.startsWith('gravity-')){s.worldChoice=op.slice(8);s.travel=0;}
 else if(op==='drop'){s.dropped.add(s.worldChoice);s.travel=0;lab.animate('travel',.78);}
 s.moves++;
}
function install(){
 const games=window.Minigames;if(!games)return;
 if(games._v23Installed){ if(games._v23StartTask && games.startTask!==games._v23StartTask) games.startTask=games._v23StartTask; return; }
 games._v23Installed=true;
 const oldStart=games.startTask.bind(games),oldClose=games.close.bind(games);
 let active=null;
 games.close=function(){if(active){active.dispose();active=null;}window.MoonshiftActiveLab=null;oldClose();};
 games.startTask=function(id,onComplete){
  const w=world(),spec=SPECS[w]?.[id];
  if(!spec)return oldStart(id,onComplete);
  if(window.Sim?.missionFailed){window.PowerCrisis?.show?.();return;}
  this.close();this.onComplete=onComplete;this.currentTaskId=w+'_'+id;
  const [kind,title,objective,lesson,disclaimer]=spec;
  const shell=document.createElement('div');shell.className='task-overlay v23-overlay';
  shell.innerHTML=`<section class="v23-lab" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="${U.escape(title)}" tabindex="-1"><header class="v23-header"><div><span class="v23-eyebrow">${w.toUpperCase()} · MISSION SCIENCE LAB</span><h2>${U.escape(title)}</h2><p>${U.escape(objective)}</p></div><button class="v23-x" aria-label="Close task" type="button">✕</button></header><nav class="v23-steps" aria-label="Task steps"><button data-phase="learn" class="is-active">01 · Understand</button><button data-phase="try">02 · Experiment</button><button data-phase="mission">03 · Mission</button></nav><div class="v23-content"><div class="v23-scene" id="v23Scene"></div><div class="v23-info"><div id="v23InfoBody"></div><div class="v23-hint" id="v23Hint" aria-live="polite">Learn, experiment, then complete the mission.</div></div></div><footer class="v23-footer"><span>↗ Game model · ${U.escape(disclaimer)}</span><div class="v23-footer-actions"><button class="v23-help" id="v23Mode" type="button"></button><button class="v23-continue" id="v23Next" type="button">TRY IT →</button></div></footer></section>`;
  document.body.appendChild(shell);this.overlayElement=shell;this.activeMinigame=title;
  const lab={w,id,kind,title,objective,lesson,disclaimer,phase:'learn',mode:getMode(),state:createState(kind),root:shell,raf:null,success:false,complete:false,
   message:(txt,type='info')=>{const h=shell.querySelector('#v23Hint');if(h){h.textContent=txt;h.dataset.type=type;}},
   dispose:()=>{if(lab.raf)cancelAnimationFrame(lab.raf);lab.raf=null;},
   animate:(prop,duration)=>{lab.dispose();const start=performance.now();const step=now=>{if(!shell.isConnected)return;const t=U.clamp((now-start)/(duration*1000),0,1);lab.state[prop]=t;const scene=shell.querySelector('#v23Scene');if(scene)scene.innerHTML=renderScene(lab);if(t<1)lab.raf=requestAnimationFrame(step);else{lab.state[prop]=1;lab.raf=null;lab.message('Visual experiment finished. Check the result!','success');}};lab.raf=requestAnimationFrame(step);}
  };
  active=lab;window.MoonshiftActiveLab=lab;
  const refreshScene=()=>{const scene=shell.querySelector('#v23Scene');if(scene){scene.innerHTML=renderScene(lab);scene.querySelectorAll('[data-panel]').forEach(cell=>{cell.style.cursor='pointer';cell.onclick=()=>{if(lab.phase==='learn')return;runOp(lab,'solar-panel-'+cell.dataset.panel);refreshScene();updateControls();}});}};
  const updateControls=()=>{
   if(lab.phase==='learn'||lab.phase==='success')return;
   const box=shell.querySelector('#v23Controls');if(!box)return;
   box.innerHTML=controls(lab)+`<div class="v30-tool-row"><button type="button" class="v30-coach" data-v30-hint>✦ Show recommended setup</button><span class="v30-coach-note">Example values are for this simulation.</span><button type="button" class="v31-energy-in-lab" data-v31-energy>⚡ Recover power</button></div>`;
   box.querySelector('[data-v31-energy]')?.addEventListener('click',()=>window.MoonshiftEnergy?.open());box.querySelector('[data-v30-hint]')?.addEventListener('click',()=>{const presets={garden:{light:8,water:2},power:{loads:{air:10,lights:2,radio:2,science:0}},signal:{angle:116,packet:'compressed',sent:true,travel:1},thermal:{temp:21},docking:{distance:14,speed:1.8,offset:0},air:{fan:true,filter:true,oxygen:true},water:{stage:4},shield:{locks:[true,true,true],manual:true},solar:{clean:[true,true,true,true,false,false],economy:true},route:{route:'safe',travel:1},gravity:{dropped:new Set(['earth',lab.w==='mars'?'mars':'moon'])},drift:{x:385,v:0}};const sample=presets[kind];if(!sample)return;Object.assign(lab.state,sample,{moves:Math.max(1,lab.state.moves||0)});refreshScene();updateControls();const r=feedback(lab);lab.message('Example loaded. '+r.why,'success');const v=shell.querySelector('#v23Result');if(v)v.textContent=r.why;});box.querySelectorAll('button[data-op]').forEach(btn=>btn.onclick=()=>{runOp(lab,btn.dataset.op);refreshScene();updateControls();const result=feedback(lab);lab.message(result.why,result.ok?'success':'info');});
   box.querySelectorAll('input[data-op]').forEach(inp=>inp.oninput=()=>{runOp(lab,inp.dataset.op,inp.value);refreshScene();const label=inp.closest('label').querySelector('b');if(label)label.textContent=`${inp.value} ${inp.dataset.op==='angle'?'°':inp.dataset.op==='water'?'L/hour':'kW'}`;const res=feedback(lab);lab.message(res.why,res.ok?'success':'info');const v=shell.querySelector('#v23Result');if(v)v.textContent=res.why;const fresh=controls(lab);if(lab.kind==='garden'&&box){const focusOp=inp.dataset.op;box.innerHTML=fresh;const replacement=box.querySelector('input[data-op="'+focusOp+'"]');if(replacement){replacement.focus({preventScroll:true});}updateControls();}});
  };
  const render=()=>{
   const right=shell.querySelector('#v23InfoBody'),next=shell.querySelector('#v23Next');
   shell.querySelectorAll('[data-phase]').forEach(b=>{b.classList.toggle('is-active',b.dataset.phase===lab.phase);b.disabled=b.dataset.phase==='mission'&&lab.phase==='learn';});
   const maxWord=lab.mode==='explorer'?'Try things freely. Mistakes are safe.':'Watch every variable and choose an efficient plan.';
   if(lab.phase==='learn'){
    right.innerHTML=`<div class="v23-card"><span class="v23-kicker">WHAT IS GOING ON?</span><h3>${U.escape(objective)}</h3><p>${U.escape(lesson)}</p></div><div class="v23-card v23-card-soft"><span class="v23-kicker">YOUR GOAL</span><p>Use the instruments on the left to test a plan. Then solve the mission and see what changes in the station.</p><span class="v23-nasa">Illustrated physics and resource values are simplified. <a href="${kind==='drift'||kind==='docking'||kind==='gravity'?LEO:kind==='signal'&&w==='mars'?MARS:SITE}" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Read NASA science ↗</a></span></div>`;
    next.textContent='TRY THE EXPERIMENT →';
   } else if(lab.phase==='success'){
    const result=feedback(lab);right.innerHTML=`<div class="v23-card v23-win"><span class="v23-kicker">MISSION COMPLETE</span><h3>Great engineering! ✦</h3><p>${U.escape(result.why)}</p><div class="v23-score">+${result.points} mission points</div><p>Only your first successful completion earns profile XP. You can replay to improve your personal best.</p></div><div class="v23-card v23-card-soft"><strong>What did we learn?</strong><p>${U.escape(lesson)}</p></div>`;next.textContent='RETURN TO STATION';
   } else {
    right.innerHTML=`<div class="v23-card"><span class="v23-kicker">${lab.phase==='try'?'EXPERIMENT · NO PENALTY':'THE REAL MISSION'}</span><h3>${lab.phase==='try'?'Play with the controls':'Solve the problem'}</h3><p>${lab.phase==='try'?'Nothing you do in this step uses station resources. Explore and try again!':U.escape(objective)}</p></div><div class="v23-controls" id="v23Controls"></div><div class="v23-card v23-preview"><span class="v23-kicker">LIVE FEEDBACK</span><p id="v23Result">${U.escape(maxWord)}</p></div>`;
    next.textContent=lab.phase==='try'?'BEGIN MISSION →':'CHECK MY SOLUTION';
   }
   shell.querySelector('#v23Mode').textContent=lab.mode==='explorer'?'Explorer · Easy':'Mission Specialist';
   refreshScene();updateControls();if(lab.phase==='try'||lab.phase==='mission'){const v=shell.querySelector('#v23Result');if(v)v.textContent=feedback(lab).why;}
   lab.message(lab.phase==='learn'?'Learn how it works, then try it.':lab.phase==='success'?'Progress saved locally. Great work!':lab.phase==='try'?'Practice mode — you cannot lose energy here.':'Every choice counts — check your result before you finish.');
  };
  shell.querySelector('.v23-x').onclick=()=>games.close();
  shell.addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target===shell)games.close();});
  shell.querySelector('#v23Mode').onclick=()=>{lab.mode=lab.mode==='explorer'?'specialist':'explorer';try{localStorage.setItem(modeKey,lab.mode)}catch(e){}render();};
  shell.querySelectorAll('[data-phase]').forEach(btn=>btn.onclick=()=>{const p=btn.dataset.phase;if(p==='mission'&&lab.phase==='learn')return;if(lab.phase==='success')return;lab.dispose();lab.phase=p;lab.state=createState(kind);render();});
  shell.querySelector('#v23Next').onclick=()=>{
   if(lab.phase==='learn'){lab.phase='try';lab.state=createState(kind);render();return;}
   if(lab.phase==='try'){lab.dispose();lab.phase='mission';lab.state=createState(kind);render();return;}
   if(lab.phase==='success'){
     const reactions={
       power:'Nice! The crew keeps essential systems powered while we watch the battery.',
       garden:'Look at the crops! Healthy plants still need water and electricity.',
       shield:'Our shelter is prepared. Good thinking, astronaut!',
       drift:'A tiny push made all the difference. Great catch!',
       docking:'Soft capture! Slow and steady wins in space.',
       air:'Fresh air is cycling again. Thanks for helping the crew!',
       water:'Our precious water is ready for recycling. Every drop counts!',
       solar:'Cleaner panels help the battery. The dust will not win today!',
       route:'What a route! The rover made it back with science to share.',
       signal:'Signal sent! Space messages need careful aiming and patience.',
       thermal:'Great temperature control. Comfortable crew, smarter power use.',
       gravity:'Different worlds, different falling times. Excellent experiment!'
     };
     games.close();window.Engine?.pix?.say?.(reactions[kind]||'Mission complete!','happy',4);return;
   }
   const outcome=feedback(lab);
   if(!outcome.ok){lab.message('Not yet! '+outcome.why,'warning');shell.querySelector('#v23Result').textContent=outcome.why;return;}
   const sim=window.Sim;
   const waitingCost=kind==='shield'&&lab.state.manual? Math.max(0,-sim.netPower)*.5:0;
   if(lab.mode!=='explorer'&&sim.batteryCharge+1e-4<outcome.cost+waitingCost){
     lab.message(`This plan may need ${fmt(outcome.cost+waitingCost,1)} kWh including the preparation period. You have ${fmt(sim.batteryCharge,1)} kWh. Restore power or choose a different method.`,'warning');
     return;
   }
   lab.dispose();sim.batteryCharge=U.clamp(sim.batteryCharge-outcome.cost,0,sim.batteryCapacityMax);
   outcome.updates();
   if(kind==='shield'&&lab.state.manual)sim.update(1800); // fictional half-hour crew preparation cost
   sim.update(0);sim.updateAlerts();
   const bestKey=keyFor(w,id);let prev=0;try{prev=Number(localStorage.getItem(bestKey))||0;localStorage.setItem(bestKey,String(Math.max(prev,outcome.points)))}catch(e){}
   window.Beyond?.award?.(`v23-${w}-${id}`,outcome.points);
   if(window.Beyond?.state?.best){
     const display=`${({moon:'Moon',orbit:'Orbit',mars:'Mars'})[w]} · ${title}`;
     window.Beyond.state.best[display]=Math.max(Number(window.Beyond.state.best[display])||0,outcome.points);
     window.Beyond.save?.();
   }
   window.Story?.decisions?.push({type:'interactiveLab',world:w,task:id,score:outcome.points,storedEnergyCostKWh:outcome.cost,missionHour:sim.missionTimeHours});
   window.Save?.schedule?.();
   lab.phase='success';lab.success=true;render();
   window.Audio?.taskSuccess?.();
   // Story progression only on the first successful objective completion.
   if(!lab.complete){lab.complete=true;onComplete?.(id);}
  };
  render();shell.querySelector('.v23-lab')?.focus();
 };
 const save=window.Save;
 if(save&&!save._v23ResetPatched){
   save._v23ResetPatched=true;
   const reset=save.reset.bind(save);
   save.reset=function(...args){
     let result=reset(...args);
     if(result){
       try{for(const [w,tasks] of Object.entries(SPECS))for(const task of Object.keys(tasks))localStorage.removeItem(keyFor(w,task));}catch(e){}
     }
     return result;
   };
 }
 games._v23StartTask=games.startTask;
 window.MoonshiftInteractiveLabs={SPECS,feedback,createState,version:'23',getMode};
}
if(document.readyState==='complete') install();
else { document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',install,{once:true}); window.addEventListener('load',install,{once:true}); }
setTimeout(install,250);
})();

</script>

.nx-choice-check{color:#a8e2cd;font-size:18px}

.nx-action{border-radius:12px;border:1px solid rgba(232,247,253,.51);background:linear-gradient(130deg,#c5eff0,#8fcbdb);color:#102939;font:850 12px/1.3 inherit;min-height:46px;padding:12px 17px;cursor:pointer;width:100%;transition:.15s;letter-spacing:.02em}

.nx-message{border:1px solid rgba(142,198,169,.36);background:rgba(28,71,59,.43);color:#c9f2db;border-radius:12px;padding:11px 13px;margin:12px 0;font-size:12px;line-height:1.45}
.nx-message[data-level=warn]{border-color:#ad825b;background:rgba(99,64,42,.4);color:#ffe0bd}
